-- 检查用户是否在白名单中
local function isWhitelisted(player)
    if not player then return false end
    -- 检查用户名
    local playerName = player.Name
    for _, whitelisted in ipairs(whitelist) do
        if whitelisted == playerName then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

        task.wait(0.3)
        whitelistGui:Destroy()
    end)
    
    return whitelistGui
end

-- 检查并执行白名单逻辑
local function checkWhitelist()
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not localPlayer then return false end
    
    local isWhitelistedUser = isWhitelisted(localPlayer)
    if not isWhitelistedUser then
        print("[Whitelist] Không có trong whitelist: ", localPlayer.Name)
        showWhitelistWarning()
        return true
    end
    
    return false
end

-- 执行白名单检查
if checkWhitelist() then
    -- 阻止脚本继续执行
    return
end

configPath = LocalPlayer.Name .. "_chi_chi_world_config.json"

-- ===================== BẢNG XẾP HẠNG SIZE NGƯỜI CHƠI (THỜI GIAN THỰC) =====================
local function formatNumberWithCommas(num)
	local n = tonumber(num) or 0
	local isNegative = n < 0
	n = math.abs(n)

	local formatted = tostring(math.floor(n))
	local k
	repeat
		formatted, k = string.gsub(formatted, "^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2")
	until k == 0

	if isNegative then
		formatted = "-" .. formatted
	end
	return formatted
end

local function getPlayerSizeValue(player)
	if not player then return 0 end

	-- 1. Kiểm tra trong leaderstats (phổ biến nhất: Size / size)
	local leaderstats = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
	if leaderstats then
		local sizeStat = leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Size") or leaderstats:FindFirstChild("size")
		if sizeStat and (sizeStat:IsA("ValueBase") or type(sizeStat) == "number") then
			local val = sizeStat:IsA("ValueBase") and sizeStat.Value or sizeStat
			return tonumber(val) or 0
		end
	end

	-- 2. Kiểm tra Attribute trên Player hoặc Character
	local attrSize = player:GetAttribute("Size") or player:GetAttribute("size")
	if attrSize == nil and player.Character then
		attrSize = player.Character:GetAttribute("Size") or player:GetAttribute("size")
	end
	if attrSize ~= nil then
		return tonumber(attrSize) or 0
	end

	-- 3. Kiểm tra ValueBase trực tiếp dưới Player hoặc Character (nếu game lưu riêng)
	local directVal = player:FindFirstChild("Size", true) or (player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Size", true))
	if directVal and directVal:IsA("ValueBase") then
		return tonumber(directVal.Value) or 0
	end

	return 0
end

local function makeDraggable(guiObject, dragHandle)
	dragHandle = dragHandle or guiObject
	local dragging = false
	local dragInput = nil
	local dragStart = nil
	local startPos = nil

	dragHandle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = true
			dragStart = input.Position
			startPos = guiObject.Position

			input.Changed:Connect(function()
				if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					dragging = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end)

	dragHandle.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragInput = input
		end
	end)

	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and input == dragInput then
			local delta = input.Position - dragStart
			guiObject.Position = UDim2.new(
				startPos.X.Scale,
				startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
				startPos.Y.Scale,
				startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
			)
		end
	end)
end

local function createSizeLeaderboard()
	local oldGui = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("PlayerSizeLeaderboardGui")
	if oldGui then
		oldGui:Destroy()
	end

	local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	screenGui.Name = "PlayerSizeLeaderboardGui"
	screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
	screenGui.Parent = CoreGui

	local ROW_HEIGHT = 20
	local MAX_VISIBLE_ROWS = 6
	local HEADER_HEIGHT = 22
	local PADDING = 5
	local listMaxHeight = ROW_HEIGHT * MAX_VISIBLE_ROWS

	-- Nút Bật / Tắt bảng xếp hạng (màu xám nhạt, cỡ chữ 10, kéo di chuyển được)
	local toggleButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	toggleButton.Name = "ToggleLeaderboardButton"
	toggleButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 22)
	toggleButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -330, 0.5, -100)
	toggleButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 230)
	toggleButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	toggleButton.Text = "BXH Size: Bật"
	toggleButton.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	toggleButton.TextSize = 10
	toggleButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 40)
	toggleButton.Parent = screenGui

	local toggleCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	toggleCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	toggleCorner.Parent = toggleButton

	local toggleStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	toggleStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(175, 175, 185)
	toggleStroke.Thickness = 1
	toggleStroke.Parent = toggleButton

	-- Khung bảng xếp hạng chính (màu xám nhạt, cỡ chữ 10)
	local mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	mainFrame.Name = "SizeLeaderboardFrame"
	mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 215, 0, HEADER_HEIGHT + listMaxHeight + PADDING * 2)
	mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -230, 0.5, -100)
	mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 230)
	mainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	mainFrame.Visible = true
	mainFrame.Parent = screenGui

	local frameCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	frameCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
	frameCorner.Parent = mainFrame

	local frameStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	frameStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(175, 175, 185)
	frameStroke.Thickness = 1
	frameStroke.Parent = mainFrame

	-- Thanh tiêu đề (kéo thanh này để di chuyển toàn bộ bảng)
	local headerBar = Instance.new("Frame")
	headerBar.Name = "HeaderBar"
	headerBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, HEADER_HEIGHT)
	headerBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 2)
	headerBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	headerBar.Parent = mainFrame

	local titleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	titleLabel.Name = "TitleLabel"
	titleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0)
	titleLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	titleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	titleLabel.Text = "BXH Size Người Chơi"
	titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	titleLabel.TextSize = 14
	titleLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 40)
	titleLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	titleLabel.Parent = headerBar

	-- Nút thu gọn/tắt nhanh ngay trên góc bảng
	local quickCloseBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	quickCloseBtn.Name = "QuickCloseBtn"
	quickCloseBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 16)
	quickCloseBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.5, -8)
	quickCloseBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 212)
	quickCloseBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	quickCloseBtn.Text = "X"
	quickCloseBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	quickCloseBtn.TextSize = 16
	quickCloseBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 45)
	quickCloseBtn.Parent = headerBar

	local closeBtnCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	closeBtnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
	closeBtnCorner.Parent = quickCloseBtn

	-- Khung cuộn danh sách (tự động có thanh cuộn khi > 6 dòng)
	local scrollFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	scrollFrame.Name = "RowsScrollFrame"
	scrollFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, listMaxHeight)
	scrollFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, HEADER_HEIGHT + 3)
	scrollFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(236, 236, 241)
	scrollFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	scrollFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 4
	scrollFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(135, 135, 145)
	scrollFrame.ScrollingEnabled = false
	scrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	scrollFrame.Parent = mainFrame

	local scrollCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	scrollCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
	scrollCorner.Parent = scrollFrame

	local listLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	listLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	listLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 0)
	listLayout.Parent = scrollFrame

	-- Đồng bộ trạng thái Bật / Tắt
	local function setLeaderboardVisible(state)
		mainFrame.Visible = state
		if state then
			toggleButton.Text = "BXH Size: Bật"
			toggleButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 230)
		else
			toggleButton.Text = "BXH Size: Tắt"
			toggleButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 212)
		end
	end

	toggleButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		setLeaderboardVisible(not mainFrame.Visible)
	end)

	quickCloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		setLeaderboardVisible(false)
	end)

	-- Cho phép kéo di chuyển cả bảng lẫn nút bật/tắt
	makeDraggable(mainFrame, headerBar)
	makeDraggable(toggleButton, toggleButton)

	local function refreshLeaderboard()
		if not mainFrame.Visible then return end

		for _, child in ipairs(scrollFrame:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Frame") then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end

		local entries = {}
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			table.insert(entries, {
				Name = plr.Name,
				SizeVal = getPlayerSizeValue(plr)
			})
		end

		table.sort(entries, function(a, b)
			if a.SizeVal == b.SizeVal then
				return a.Name < b.Name
			end
			return a.SizeVal > b.SizeVal
		end)

		local totalRows = #entries
		scrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, totalRows * ROW_HEIGHT)
		scrollFrame.ScrollingEnabled = totalRows > MAX_VISIBLE_ROWS

		for idx, item in ipairs(entries) do
			local row = Instance.new("Frame")
			row.Name = "Row_" .. item.Name
			row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ROW_HEIGHT)
			row.LayoutOrder = idx
			row.BackgroundColor3 = (idx % 2 == 0) and Color3.fromRGB(228, 228, 234) or Color3.fromRGB(238, 238, 243)
			row.BorderSizePixel = 0
			row.Parent = scrollFrame

			local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.56, -5, 1, 0)
			nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 0)
			nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			nameLabel.Text = string.format("#%d %s", idx, item.Name)
			nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
			nameLabel.TextSize = 15
			nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 40)
			nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			nameLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
			nameLabel.Parent = row

			local sizeLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			sizeLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.44, -5, 1, 0)
			sizeLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.56, 0, 0, 0)
			sizeLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			sizeLabel.Text = formatNumberWithCommas(item.SizeVal)
			sizeLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
			sizeLabel.TextSize = 15
			sizeLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 55)
			sizeLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
			sizeLabel.Parent = row
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while screenGui.Parent do
			refreshLeaderboard()
			task.wait(0.25)
		end
	end)
end

createSizeLeaderboard()
-- ===================== ÁP DỤNG MÀU BẢNG GIỐNG HÌNH =====================
local function applyDarkLeaderboardTheme()
	local gui = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("PlayerSizeLeaderboardGui")
	if not gui then return end

	local toggleBtn = gui:FindFirstChild("ToggleLeaderboardButton")
	local mainFrame = gui:FindFirstChild("SizeLeaderboardFrame")
	local headerBar = mainFrame and mainFrame:FindFirstChild("HeaderBar")
	local titleLabel = headerBar and headerBar:FindFirstChild("TitleLabel")
	local quickCloseBtn = headerBar and headerBar:FindFirstChild("QuickCloseBtn")
	local scrollFrame = mainFrame and mainFrame:FindFirstChild("RowsScrollFrame")

	local darkBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(32, 30, 30)
	local darkBorderColor = Color3.fromRGB(95, 90, 90)
	local dividerColor = Color3.fromRGB(115, 110, 110)
	local lightTextColor = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)

	if toggleBtn then
		toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = darkBgColor
		toggleBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
		toggleBtn.TextColor3 = lightTextColor
		local tStroke = toggleBtn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
		if tStroke then
			tStroke.Color = darkBorderColor
			tStroke.Transparency = 0.35
		end
	end

	if mainFrame then
		mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = darkBgColor
		mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
		local mStroke = mainFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
		if mStroke then
			mStroke.Color = darkBorderColor
			mStroke.Transparency = 0.35
		end
	end

	if titleLabel then
		titleLabel.TextColor3 = lightTextColor
	end

	if quickCloseBtn then
		quickCloseBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 45, 45)
		quickCloseBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
		quickCloseBtn.TextColor3 = lightTextColor
	end

	if scrollFrame then
		scrollFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		scrollFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 145)

		local function styleRow(row)
			if not row:IsA("Frame") then return end
			row.BackgroundTransparency = 1

			for _, child in ipairs(row:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("TextLabel") then
					child.TextColor3 = lightTextColor
				end
			end

			if not row:FindFirstChild("RowDivider") then
				local divider = Instance.new("Frame")
				divider.Name = "RowDivider"
				divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
				divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1)
				divider.BackgroundColor3 = dividerColor
				divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
				divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
				divider.Parent = row
			end
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(scrollFrame:GetChildren()) do
			styleRow(child)
		end

		if not scrollFrame:GetAttribute("ThemeHooked") then
			scrollFrame:SetAttribute("ThemeHooked", true)
			scrollFrame.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				task.wait()
				styleRow(child)
			end)
		end
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	while CoreGui:FindFirstChild("PlayerSizeLeaderboardGui") do
		applyDarkLeaderboardTheme()
		task.wait(0.25)
	end
end)
-- ===================== 配置管理 =====================
local config = {
    -- 自动功能
    autofarm = false,
    autoCollectingCubes = false,
    autoClaimRewards = false,
    farmMoving = false,
    showMap = false,
    autoeat = false,
    
    -- 人物功能
    keepUnanchor = false,
    antiFallEnabled = false,
    slippageEliminationEnabled = false,
    gravityLockEnabled = false,
    
    -- 其他功能
    platformEnabled = false
}

-- 保存配置
local function saveConfig()
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local configData = HttpService:JSONEncode(config)
    
    -- 尝试保存到PlayerGui
    local configFolder = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ChiChiWorldConfig")
    if not configFolder then
        configFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
        configFolder.Name = "ChiChiWorldConfig"
        configFolder.Parent = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui
    end
    
    local configFile = configFolder:FindFirstChild(configPath)
    if not configFile then
        configFile = Instance.new("StringValue")
        configFile.Name = configPath
        configFile.Parent = configFolder
    end
    
    configFile.Value = configData
    print("[Cấu hình] Đã lưu cài đặt")
end

-- 加载配置
local function loadConfig()
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    
    -- 尝试从PlayerGui加载
    local configFolder = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ChiChiWorldConfig")
    if configFolder then
        local configFile = configFolder:FindFirstChild(configPath)
        if configFile and configFile.Value ~= "" then
            local success, configData = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(configFile.Value)
            end)
            
            if success and configData then
                -- 更新配置
                for key, value in pairs(configData) do
                    if config[key] ~= nil then
                        config[key] = value
                    end
                end
                print("[Cấu hình] Đã tải cài đặt")
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    
    print("[Cấu hình] Không có tệp, dùng mặc định")
    return false
end

-- 自动保存配置（当设置改变时）
local function autoSaveConfig()
    saveConfig()
end

-- 加载配置
loadConfig()

-- ===================== 本地化UI库实现 =====================
local UI = {}
UI.__index = UI

-- 创建主窗口
function UI:NewWindow(title)
    local self = setmetatable({}, UI)
    
    -- 主容器
    self.MainGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    self.MainGui.Name = "ChiChiWorldUI"
    self.MainGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    self.MainGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    self.MainGui.Parent = syn and CoreGui or PlayerGui
    
    -- 主窗口框架
    self.Window = Instance.new("Frame")
    self.Window.Name = "MainWindow"
    self.Window.Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 88)
    self.Window.Position = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 0.1, 0)
    self.Window.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    self.Window.BorderSizePixel = 0
    self.Window.ClipsDescendants = true
    self.Window.Parent = self.MainGui
    
    -- 窗口圆角
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    corner.Parent = self.Window
    
    -- 标题栏
    self.TitleBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    self.TitleBar.Name = "TitleBar"
    self.TitleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
    self.TitleBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 42)
    self.TitleBar.Parent = self.Window
    
    -- 标题文字
    self.TitleText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    self.TitleText.Name = "TitleText"
    self.TitleText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
    self.TitleText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    self.TitleText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    self.TitleText.Text = title
    self.TitleText.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    self.TitleText.TextSize = 16
    self.TitleText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    self.TitleText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    self.TitleText.Parent = self.TitleBar
    
    -- 收起按钮
    self.CollapseBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    self.CollapseBtn.Name = "CollapseBtn"
    self.CollapseBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 1, 0)
    self.CollapseBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 0)
    self.CollapseBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    self.CollapseBtn.Text = "-"
    self.CollapseBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    self.CollapseBtn.TextSize = 18
    self.CollapseBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    self.CollapseBtn.Parent = self.TitleBar
    
    -- 内容容器
    self.ContentContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    self.ContentContainer.Name = "ContentContainer"
    self.ContentContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -26)
    self.ContentContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 26)
    self.ContentContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    self.ContentContainer.ClipsDescendants = false
    self.ContentContainer.Parent = self.Window
    
    -- 菜单容器
    self.MenuContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    self.MenuContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    self.MenuContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    self.MenuContainer.ClipsDescendants = false
    self.MenuContainer.Parent = self.ContentContainer
    
    -- 窗口拖拽功能
    self:MakeDraggable()
    
    -- 收起/展开功能
    self.IsCollapsed = false
    self.CollapseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        self:ToggleCollapse()
    end)
    
    -- 存储所有子菜单
    self.Sections = {}
    self.CurrentY = 0
    
    return self
end

-- 窗口拖拽
function UI:MakeDraggable()
    local dragging = false
    local dragStart = Vector2.new()
    local startPos = UDim2.new()
    
    self.TitleBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = self.Window.Position
            UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = false
        end
    end)
    
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            self.Window.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale,
                startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale,
                startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end)
    
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
            UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
        end
    end)
end

-- 收起/展开窗口
function UI:ToggleCollapse()
    self.IsCollapsed = not self.IsCollapsed
    
    if self.IsCollapsed then
        -- 收起
        -- 同步收起所有展开的子菜单
        for _, section in ipairs(self.Sections) do
            if section.IsExpanded then
                section.IsExpanded = false
                section.ToggleBtn.Text = "+"
                TweenService:Create(section.Content, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                }):Play()
                -- 更新主容器高度
                self.CurrentY = self.CurrentY - section.ContentY
            end
        end
        
        TweenService:Create(self.Window, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 26)
        }):Play()
        self.CollapseBtn.Text = "+"
        self.ContentContainer.Visible = false
    else
        -- 展开
        -- 重新计算CurrentY和所有子菜单的位置
        self.CurrentY = 0
        local currentOffset = 0
        for _, section in ipairs(self.Sections) do
            -- 设置子菜单的位置
            section.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, currentOffset)
            section.Content.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, currentOffset + 36)
            
            -- 更新当前偏移量和CurrentY
            currentOffset = currentOffset + 36
            self.CurrentY = self.CurrentY + 36
        end
        
        -- 计算正确的窗口高度，与子菜单展开/收起时的逻辑一致
        local newHeight = math.max(88, 26 + self.CurrentY + 6) -- 26 是标题栏高度，6 是底部边距
        TweenService:Create(self.Window, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, newHeight)
        }):Play()
        self.CollapseBtn.Text = "-"
        -- 确保MenuContainer大小正确
        self.MenuContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        -- 恢复ContentContainer的相对大小
        self.ContentContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -26)
        -- 确保所有子菜单都可见
        for _, section in ipairs(self.Sections) do
            section.Frame.Visible = true
            section.Content.Visible = true
        end
        task.wait(0.2)
        self.ContentContainer.Visible = true
    end
end

-- 创建子菜单
function UI:NewSection(title)
    local section = {}
    section.Title = title
    section.Frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    section.Frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
    section.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, self.CurrentY)
    section.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 42)
    section.Frame.Parent = self.MenuContainer
    
    -- 子菜单圆角
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    corner.Parent = section.Frame
    
    -- 子菜单标题
    section.TitleText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    section.TitleText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
    section.TitleText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    section.TitleText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    section.TitleText.Text = title
    section.TitleText.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    section.TitleText.TextSize = 14
    section.TitleText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    section.TitleText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    section.TitleText.Parent = section.Frame
    
    -- 展开/收起按钮
    section.ToggleBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    section.ToggleBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 1, 0)
    section.ToggleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 0)
    section.ToggleBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    section.ToggleBtn.Text = "+"
    section.ToggleBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    section.ToggleBtn.TextSize = 16
    section.ToggleBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    section.ToggleBtn.Parent = section.Frame
    
    -- 功能按钮容器
    section.Content = Instance.new("Frame")
    section.Content.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    section.Content.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, self.CurrentY + 36)
    section.Content.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    section.Content.ClipsDescendants = true
    section.Content.Parent = self.MenuContainer
    
    -- 状态
    section.IsExpanded = false
    section.Buttons = {}
    section.ContentY = 0
    
    -- 更新菜单容器高度
    self.CurrentY = self.CurrentY + 36
    self.MenuContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, self.CurrentY)
    
    -- 确保子菜单可见
    section.Frame.Visible = true
    section.Content.Visible = true
    
    -- 更新主窗口高度以跟随子菜单大小
    local newHeight = math.max(150, 40 + self.CurrentY + 20) -- 40 是标题栏高度，20 是底部边距
    TweenService:Create(self.Window, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, newHeight)
    }):Play()
    
    -- 更新内容容器大小
    self.ContentContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -40)
    
    -- 子菜单展开/收起
    section.ToggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        section.IsExpanded = not section.IsExpanded
        
        if section.IsExpanded then
            -- 展开
            section.ToggleBtn.Text = "-"
            TweenService:Create(section.Content, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, section.ContentY)
            }):Play()
            
            -- 更新主容器高度
            self.CurrentY = self.CurrentY + section.ContentY
        else
            -- 收起
            section.ToggleBtn.Text = "+"
            TweenService:Create(section.Content, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            }):Play()
            
            -- 更新主容器高度
            self.CurrentY = self.CurrentY - section.ContentY
        end
        
        -- 重新计算所有子菜单的位置
        local currentOffset = 0
        for i, sec in ipairs(self.Sections) do
            -- 设置子菜单的位置
            sec.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, currentOffset)
            sec.Content.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, currentOffset + 36)
            
            -- 更新当前偏移量
            currentOffset = currentOffset + 36
            if sec.IsExpanded then
                currentOffset = currentOffset + sec.ContentY
            end
        end
        
        -- 保持MenuContainer大小不变，确保所有子菜单都能显示
        self.MenuContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        
        -- 更新主窗口高度以跟随子菜单大小
        local newHeight = math.max(88, 26 + self.CurrentY + 6) -- 26 是标题栏高度，6 是底部边距
        TweenService:Create(self.Window, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, newHeight)
        }):Play()
        
        -- 确保内容容器大小正确
        self.ContentContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -26)
        
        -- 确保所有子菜单都可见
        for _, sec in ipairs(self.Sections) do
            sec.Frame.Visible = true
            sec.Content.Visible = true
        end
    end)
    
    -- 创建开关按钮
    function section:CreateToggle(text, callback)
        -- 计算按钮位置
        local isEven = #section.Buttons % 2 == 0
        local btnSize = UDim2.new(0.50, 0, 0, 26)
        local btnPosition
        
        if isEven then
            btnPosition = UDim2.new(0.50, 0, 0, section.ContentY)
        else
            btnPosition = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, section.ContentY)
        end
        
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Name = "ToggleBtn_" .. text
        btn.Size = btnSize
        btn.Position = btnPosition
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 50)
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        btn.Parent = section.Content
        
        -- 按钮圆角
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        corner.Parent = btn
        
        -- 按钮文字
        local btnText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        btnText.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        btnText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        btnText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        btnText.Text = text
        btnText.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
        btnText.TextSize = 15
        btnText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        btnText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        btnText.Parent = btn
        
        -- 开关状态
        local toggleState = false
        
        -- 按钮点击事件
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            toggleState = not toggleState
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = toggleState and Color3.fromRGB(60, 180, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 50)
            callback(toggleState)
        end)
        
        -- 按钮悬停效果
        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
                BackgroundColor3 = toggleState and Color3.fromRGB(70, 190, 70) or Color3.fromRGB(54, 54, 60)
            }):Play()
        end)
        
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
                BackgroundColor3 = toggleState and Color3.fromRGB(60, 180, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 50)
            }):Play()
        end)
        
        -- 更新容器高度
        if not isEven then
            section.ContentY = section.ContentY + 26
        end
        table.insert(section.Buttons, btn)
        
        return btn
    end
    
    -- 创建普通按钮
    function section:CreateButton(text, callback)
        -- 计算按钮位置
        local isEven = #section.Buttons % 2 == 0
        local btnSize = UDim2.new(0.50, 0, 0, 26)
        local btnPosition
        
        if isEven then
            btnPosition = UDim2.new(0.50, 0, 0, section.ContentY)
        else
            btnPosition = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, section.ContentY)
        end
        
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Name = "Button_" .. text
        btn.Size = btnSize
        btn.Position = btnPosition
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 50)
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        btn.Text = ""
        btn.Parent = section.Content
        
        -- 按钮圆角
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        corner.Parent = btn
        
        -- 按钮文字
        local btnText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        btnText.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        btnText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        btnText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        btnText.Text = text
        btnText.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
        btnText.TextSize = 15
        btnText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        btnText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        btnText.Parent = btn
        
        -- 按钮点击事件
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            callback()
        end)
        
        -- 按钮悬停效果
        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(54, 54, 60)
            }):Play()
        end)
        
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 50)
            }):Play()
        end)
        
        -- 更新容器高度
        if not isEven then
            section.ContentY = section.ContentY + 26
        end
        table.insert(section.Buttons, btn)
        
        return btn
    end
    
    table.insert(self.Sections, section)
    return section
end

-- 消息提示函数
function UI:Message(Title, Text, Time)
    local notification = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    notification.Name = "Notification_" .. math.random(1000, 9999)
    notification.Parent = syn and CoreGui or PlayerGui
    
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 80)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -310, 0, 10 + (#notification.Parent:GetChildren() * 90))
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28)
    frame.Parent = notification
    
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    corner.Parent = frame
    
    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 25)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = Title
    title.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    title.TextSize = 16
    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    title.Parent = frame
    
    local text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    text.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -25)
    text.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 25)
    text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    text.Text = Text
    text.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
    text.TextSize = 14
    text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    text.TextWrapped = true
    text.Parent = frame
    
    -- 淡入动画
    TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
        Transparency = 0
    }):Play()
    
    -- 自动关闭
    task.delay(Time or 3, function()
        TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {
            Transparency = 1
        }):Play()
        task.wait(0.3)
        notification:Destroy()
    end)
end

character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

-- 监听角色重生事件
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
    character = newCharacter
    character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end)



-- ===================== 工具函数 =====================
local function Message(Title, Text, Time)
    if mainUI then
        mainUI:Message(Title, Text, Time)
    else
        -- 当mainUI未初始化时使用Roblox默认通知系统
        game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = Title,
            Text = Text,
            Duration = Time or 3
        })
    end
end

local function getRespawnPosition()
    local baseplate = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Baseplate")
    if baseplate then
        return baseplate.Position + Vector3.new(0, 40, 0), baseplate.Position.Y
    else
        return Vector3.new(0, 40, 0), 0
    end
end

    -- 显示/隐藏收藏的动作
    local function ToggleFavorites()
        local showFavorites = not FavoritesFrame.Visible
        FavoritesFrame.Visible = showFavorites
        Open.Text = "Open"
        
        -- 过滤动作按钮
        for _, button in pairs(Frame:GetChildren()) do
            if button:IsA("ImageButton") and button.Name ~= "Random" then
                local emoteId = tonumber(button.Name)
                local isFavorited = emoteId and table.find(FavoritedEmotes, emoteId)
                button.Visible = not showFavorites or isFavorited
            end
        end
    end

    -- 创建搜索栏
    local SearchBar = Instance.new("TextBox")
    SearchBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    SearchBar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

    SearchBar.TextScaled = true

    SearchBar.Parent = BackFrame

    -- 搜索功能
    SearchBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        local text = SearchBar.Text:lower()
        local buttons = Frame:GetChildren()
        
        -- 限制搜索文本长度
        if text ~= text:sub(1, 50) then
            SearchBar.Text = SearchBar.Text:sub(1, 50)
            text = SearchBar.Text:lower()
        end
        
        -- 过滤动作按钮
        for _, button in pairs(buttons) do
            if button:IsA("GuiButton") then
                local name = button:GetAttribute("name")
                if name then
                    if text == "" or name:lower():match(text) then
                        button.Visible = true
                    else
                        button.Visible = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 打开动作菜单的函数
    local function openEmotesMenu(name, state, input)
        if state == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
            BackFrame.Visible = not BackFrame.Visible
            Open.Text = "Mở"
        end
    end

    -- 绑定逗号键打开菜单
    ContextActionService:BindCoreActionAtPriority(
        "Emote Menu",
        openEmotesMenu,
        true,
        2001,
        Enum.KeyCode.Comma
    )

    -- 处理输入
    local inputConnect
    ScreenGui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
        if BackFrame.Visible == false then
            EmoteName.Text = "Select an Emote"
            SearchBar.Text = ""
            GuiService:SetEmotesMenuOpen(false)
            
            inputConnect = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
                if not processed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    BackFrame.Visible = false
                    Open.Text = "Open"
                end
            end)
        else
            if inputConnect then
                inputConnect:Disconnect()
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 处理系统表情菜单打开
    GuiService.EmotesMenuOpenChanged:Connect(function(isOpen)
        if isOpen then
            BackFrame.Visible = false
            Open.Text = "Open"
        end
    end)

    -- 处理系统菜单打开
    GuiService.MenuOpened:Connect(function()
        BackFrame.Visible = false
        Open.Text = "Open"
    end)

    if not game:IsLoaded() then
        game.Loaded:Wait()

    -- 处理不同的执行环境
    local SynV3 = syn and DrawingImmediate
    if (not is_sirhurt_closure) and (not SynV3) and (syn and syn.protect_gui) then
        syn.protect_gui(ScreenGui)
        ScreenGui.Parent = CoreGui
    elseif get_hidden_gui or gethui then
        local hiddenUI = get_hidden_gui or gethui
        ScreenGui.Parent = hiddenUI()
    else
        ScreenGui.Parent = CoreGui
    end

    -- 发送通知函数
    local function SendNotification(title, text)
        if syn and syn.toast_notification then
            syn.toast_notification({
                Type = ToastType.Error,
                Title = title,
                Content = text
            })
        else
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = title,
                Text = text
            })
        end
    end

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    -- 播放动作函数
    local function PlayEmote(name, id)
        BackFrame.Visible = false
        Open.Text = "Open"
        SearchBar.Text = ""
        
        local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not Character then return end
        
        local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local Description = Humanoid and Humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("HumanoidDescription")
        
        if not Description then
            SendNotification("Error", "HumanoidDescription not found")
            return
        end
        
        if Humanoid.RigType ~= Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local success, err = pcall(function()
                Humanoid:PlayEmoteAndGetAnimTrackById(id)
            end)
            
            if not success then
                local addSuccess, addErr = pcall(function()
                    Description:AddEmote(name, id)
                    Humanoid:PlayEmoteAndGetAnimTrackById(id)
                end)
                
                if not addSuccess then
                    SendNotification("Error", "Failed to play emote: " .. tostring(addErr))
                end
            end
        else
            SendNotification("r6? lol", "you gotta be r15 dude")
        end
    end

    -- 等待子对象加载
    local function WaitForChildOfClass(parent, class)
        local child = parent:FindFirstChildOfClass(class)
        while not child or child.ClassName ~= class do
            child = parent.ChildAdded:Wait()
        end
        return child
    end

    -- 添加动作函数
    local function addEmote(name, id, price, date)
        local months = {
            Jan = 1, Feb = 2, Mar = 3, Apr = 4, May = 5, Jun = 6,
            Jul = 7, Aug = 8, Sep = 9, Oct = 10, Nov = 11, Dec = 12
        }
        
        local function dateToUnix(d)
            local mon, day, year = d:match("(%a+)%s+(%d+),%s*(%d+)")
            if not mon or not day or not year then return os.time() end
            return os.time({
                year = tonumber(year),
                month = months[mon],
                day = tonumber(day),
                hour = 0,
                min = 0,
                sec = 0
            })
        end
        
        table.insert(Emotes, {
            name = name,
            id = id,
            icon = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. id .. "&w=150&h=150",
            price = price or 0,
            lastupdated = dateToUnix(date),
            sort = {}
        })
    end

    -- 等待动作加载完成
    local function EmotesLoaded()
        for _, loaded in pairs(LoadedEmotes) do
            if not loaded then
                return false
            end
        end
        return true
    end

    while not EmotesLoaded() do
        task.wait()
    end

    -- 移除加载提示
    Loading:Destroy()

    -- 按名称排序动作
    table.sort(Emotes, function(a, b)
        return a.name:lower() < b.name:lower()
    end)

    -- 加载收藏的动作
    if isfile("FavoritedEmotes.txt") then
        local success, data = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile("FavoritedEmotes.txt"))
        end)
        
        if success and data then
            FavoritedEmotes = data
        else
            FavoritedEmotes = {}
        end
    else
        writefile("FavoritedEmotes.txt", HttpService:JSONEncode(FavoritedEmotes))
    end

    -- 更新收藏的动作
    local UpdatedFavorites = {}
    for _, id in pairs(FavoritedEmotes) do
        if typeof(id) == "number" then
            table.insert(UpdatedFavorites, id)
        elseif typeof(id) == "string" then
            -- 兼容旧格式
            for _, emote in pairs(Emotes) do
                if emote.name == id then
                    table.insert(UpdatedFavorites, emote.id)
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if #UpdatedFavorites > 0 then
        FavoritedEmotes = UpdatedFavorites
        writefile("FavoritedEmotes.txt", HttpService:JSONEncode(FavoritedEmotes))
    end

    -- 角色加载时创建动作按钮
    local function CharacterAdded(Character)
        -- 清理旧按钮
        for _, v in pairs(Frame:GetChildren()) do
            if not v:IsA("UIGridLayout") then
                v:Destroy()
            end
        end
        
        local Humanoid = WaitForChildOfClass(Character, "Humanoid")
        local Description = Humanoid:WaitForChild("HumanoidDescription", 5) or Instance.new("HumanoidDescription", Humanoid)
        
        -- 创建随机动作按钮
        local randomButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
        local ratio = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
        ratio.AspectType = Enum.AspectType.ScaleWithParentSize
        ratio.Parent = randomButton
        
        randomButton.LayoutOrder = 0
        randomButton.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        randomButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
        randomButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
        randomButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        randomButton.TextScaled = true
        randomButton.Text = "Random"
        randomButton:SetAttribute("name", "")
        Corner:Clone().Parent = randomButton
        
        randomButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if #Emotes > 0 then
                local randomEmote = Emotes[math.random(1, #Emotes)]
                PlayEmote(randomEmote.name, randomEmote.id)
            end
        end)
        
        randomButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            EmoteName.Text = "Random"
        end)
        
        randomButton.Parent = Frame
        
        -- 创建动作按钮
        for i, Emote in pairs(Emotes) do
            pcall(function()
                Description:AddEmote(Emote.name, Emote.id)
            end)
            
            local EmoteButton = Instance.new("ImageButton")
            local IsFavorited = table.find(FavoritedEmotes, Emote.id)
            
            EmoteButton.LayoutOrder = i + ((IsFavorited and 0) or #Emotes)
            EmoteButton.Name = tostring(Emote.id)
            EmoteButton:SetAttribute("name", Emote.name)
            Corner:Clone().Parent = EmoteButton
            EmoteButton.Image = Emote.icon
            EmoteButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
            EmoteButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
            EmoteButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
            ratio:Clone().Parent = EmoteButton
            
            -- 创建动作编号
            local EmoteNumber = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            EmoteNumber.Name = "number"
            EmoteNumber.TextScaled = true
            EmoteNumber.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            EmoteNumber.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            EmoteNumber.BorderSizePixel = 0
            EmoteNumber.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            EmoteNumber.Size = UDim2.new(0.2, 0, 0.2, 0)
            EmoteNumber.Position = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 0.9, 0)
            EmoteNumber.Text = tostring(i)
            EmoteNumber.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            EmoteNumber.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
            
            local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            UIStroke.Transparency = 0.5
            UIStroke.Parent = EmoteNumber
            EmoteNumber.Parent = EmoteButton
            
            EmoteButton.Parent = Frame
            
            -- 播放动作
            EmoteButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                PlayEmote(Emote.name, Emote.id)
            end)
            
            -- 显示动作名称
            EmoteButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                EmoteName.Text = Emote.name
            end)
            
            -- 创建收藏按钮
            local Favorite = Instance.new("ImageButton")
            Favorite.Name = "favorite"
            Favorite.Image = IsFavorited and FavoriteOn or FavoriteOff
            Favorite.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            Favorite.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0.4, 0)
            Favorite.Position = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.9, 0)
            Favorite.BorderSizePixel = 0
            Favorite.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            Favorite.Parent = EmoteButton
            
            -- 收藏功能
            Favorite.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local index = table.find(FavoritedEmotes, Emote.id)
                if index then
                    table.remove(FavoritedEmotes, index)
                    Favorite.Image = FavoriteOff
                    EmoteButton.LayoutOrder = i + #Emotes
                else
                    table.insert(FavoritedEmotes, Emote.id)
                    Favorite.Image = FavoriteOn
                    EmoteButton.LayoutOrder = i
                end
                
                -- 保存收藏
                pcall(function()
                    writefile("FavoritedEmotes.txt", HttpService:JSONEncode(FavoritedEmotes))
                end)
                
                -- 如果当前显示的是收藏列表，更新显示
                if FavoritesFrame.Visible then
                    EmoteButton.Visible = table.find(FavoritedEmotes, Emote.id) ~= nil
                end
            end)
        end
        
        -- 添加填充按钮
        for i = 1, 9 do
            local filler = Instance.new("Frame")
            filler.LayoutOrder = 2147483647
            filler.Name = "filler"
            filler.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            filler.BorderSizePixel = 0
            ratio:Clone().Parent = filler
            filler.Visible = true
            filler.Parent = Frame
            
            filler.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                EmoteName.Text = "Select an Emote"
            end)
        end
    end

    -- 处理角色加载
    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        CharacterAdded(LocalPlayer.Character)
    end

    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(CharacterAdded)

    wait(1)

    -- 启用Emotes GUI
    if game.CoreGui:FindFirstChild("Emotes") then
        game.CoreGui.Emotes.Enabled = true
    end

    -- 显示完成通知
    game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
        Title = "Done!",
        Text = "Emotes gui is here!",
        Duration = 10
    })

    -- 清理ContextActionGui
    pcall(function()
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.ContextActionGui:Destroy()
    end)
end

-- ===================== 功能实现 =====================
-- 防踢
do
    LocalPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()
        VirtualUser:CaptureController()
        VirtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
        task.wait(2)
    end)
end



-- 掉落拉回
local function startAntiFall(character)
    if antiFallConnection and antiFallConnection.Connected then
        antiFallConnection:Disconnect()
    end

    antiFallConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not antiFallEnabled then return end
        if not character or not character.Parent then return end

        local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end

        local respawnPos, baseY = getRespawnPosition()
        if root.Position.Y < baseY then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(respawnPos))
        end
    end)
end

local function stopAntiFall()
    if antiFallConnection and antiFallConnection.Connected then
        antiFallConnection:Disconnect()
        antiFallConnection = nil
    end
end

-- 速度修改
local function CreateSpeedUI()
    if SpeedUI then
        SpeedUI:Destroy()
        SpeedUI = nil
    end

    local darkBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(32, 30, 30)
    local headerBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(40, 38, 38)
    local btnBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(48, 46, 46)
    local btnHoverColor = Color3.fromRGB(64, 60, 60)
    local btnActiveColor = Color3.fromRGB(75, 165, 95)
    local borderColor = Color3.fromRGB(95, 90, 90)
    local textLightColor = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
    local subTextColor = Color3.fromRGB(195, 195, 202)

    local XenoUI = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    XenoUI.Name = "XenoSpeedHax_CR"
    XenoUI.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    XenoUI.ResetOnSpawn = false
    XenoUI.Enabled = false
    XenoUI.Parent = syn and CoreGui or PlayerGui

    local MainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    MainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = darkBgColor
    MainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    MainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    MainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 218, 0, 140)
    MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -210, 0.5, -120)
    MainFrame.Active = true
    MainFrame.Draggable = true
    MainFrame.Parent = XenoUI

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    corner.Parent = MainFrame

    local shadow = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    shadow.Color = borderColor
    shadow.Transparency = 0.35
    shadow.Thickness = 1
    shadow.Parent = MainFrame

    local TitleBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    TitleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
    TitleBar.BackgroundColor3 = headerBgColor
    TitleBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    TitleBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    TitleBar.Parent = MainFrame

    local TitleText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    TitleText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
    TitleText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    TitleText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TitleText.Text = "Speed"
    TitleText.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    TitleText.TextSize = 18
    TitleText.TextColor3 = textLightColor
    TitleText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    TitleText.Parent = TitleBar

    local CloseBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    CloseBtn.Text = "×"
    CloseBtn.TextSize = 20
    CloseBtn.TextColor3 = subTextColor
    CloseBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    CloseBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 1, 0)
    CloseBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 0)
    CloseBtn.Parent = TitleBar
    CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        XenoUI.Enabled = not XenoUI.Enabled
    end)

    local ContentFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    ContentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -50)
    ContentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 44)
    ContentFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ContentFrame.Parent = MainFrame

    -- Khung cuộn: cao 90px để hiển thị các hàng tốc độ và kéo bằng thanh cuộn
    local ScrollFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    ScrollFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 86)
    ScrollFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    ScrollFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ScrollFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ScrollFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    ScrollFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 168)
    ScrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 60)
    ScrollFrame.Parent = ContentFrame

    local speedValues = {50, 100, 150, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600, 700, 800, 850, 900, 950, 1000, 1500, 2000, 4000, 5000, 5500, 6000, 6500, 7000, 7500, 8000, 8500, 9000, 9500, 10000}
    local speedButtons = {}

    local function ApplySpeed()
        if not LocalPlayer.Character then return end

        local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then
            pcall(function()
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = CurrentSpeed
            end)
        end
    end

    local function createSpeedButton(value, row, col)
        local isSelected = (CurrentSpeed == value)
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 25)
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, (col - 1) * 50, 0, (row - 1) * 30)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = isSelected and btnActiveColor or btnBgColor
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = isSelected and 0.15 or 0.25
        btn.TextColor3 = isSelected and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or subTextColor
        btn.Text = tostring(value)
        btn.Font = isSelected and Enum.Font.SourceSansBold or Enum.Font.SourceSans
        btn.TextSize = 15
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Parent = ScrollFrame

        local btnCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        btnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        btnCorner.Parent = btn

        local btnStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        btnStroke.Color = borderColor
        btnStroke.Transparency = 0.45
        btnStroke.Thickness = 1
        btnStroke.Parent = btn

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            CurrentSpeed = value
            ApplySpeed()
            Message("Speed", "Đã áp dụng tốc độ: " .. CurrentSpeed, 2)

            for _, b in ipairs(speedButtons) do
                b.BackgroundColor3 = btnBgColor
                b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
                b.TextColor3 = subTextColor
                b.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
            end
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = btnActiveColor
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            btn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        end)

        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if CurrentSpeed ~= value then
                TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
                    BackgroundColor3 = btnHoverColor
                }):Play()
            end
        end)

        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if CurrentSpeed ~= value then
                TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
                    BackgroundColor3 = btnBgColor
                }):Play()
            end
        end)

        table.insert(speedButtons, btn)
    end

    local totalRows = math.ceil(#speedValues / 4)
    ScrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, totalRows * 30)

    for i, value in ipairs(speedValues) do
        local row = math.ceil(i / 4)
        local col = (i - 1) % 4 + 1
        createSpeedButton(value, row, col)
    end

    local function MonitorHumanoid(humanoid)
        if HumanoidTracker == humanoid then return end
        HumanoidTracker = humanoid
    end

    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        MonitorHumanoid(humanoid)
        ApplySpeed()
    end)

    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then
            MonitorHumanoid(humanoid)
            ApplySpeed()
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(1) do
            pcall(ApplySpeed)
            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid and humanoid ~= HumanoidTracker then
                    MonitorHumanoid(humanoid)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    SpeedUI = XenoUI
    return XenoUI
end

local function ToggleSpeedUI()
    if not SpeedUI then
        CreateSpeedUI()
    end
    SpeedUI.Enabled = not SpeedUI.Enabled
    local status = SpeedUI.Enabled and "Mở" or "Đóng"
end-- 取消锚固
local function cancelAnchored()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if character then
        for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.Anchored = false
            end
        end
        Message("Bỏ neo", "Đã bỏ neo toàn bộ bộ phận", 2)
    else
        Message("Bỏ neo", "Không tìm thấy nhân vật", 3)
    end
end
-- 查看玩家数据（GUI 面板）
local playerDataUI = nil

local function buildPlayerDataUI()
    -- 本地化表
    local localization = {
        MaxSize = "Kích thước",
        Speed = "Tốc độ",
        Multiplier = "Hệ số",
        EatSpeed = "Tốc độ ăn",
    }

    -- 成长函数
    local function sizeGrowth(level)
        return math.floor(((level + 0.5) ^ 2 - 0.25) / 2 * 100)
    end
    local function speedGrowth(level)
        return math.floor(level * 2 + 10)
    end
    local function multiplierGrowth(level)
        return math.floor(level)
    end
    local function eatSpeedGrowth(level)
        return math.floor((1 + (level - 1) * 0.2) * 10) / 10
    end

    -- 价格函数
    local function sizePrice(level)
        return math.floor(level ^ 3 / 2) * 20
    end
    local function speedPrice(level)
        return math.floor((level * 3) ^ 3 / 200) * 1000
    end
    local function multiplierPrice(level)
        return math.floor((level * 10) ^ 3 / 200) * 1000
    end
    local function eatSpeedPrice(level)
        return math.floor((level * 10) ^ 3 / 200) * 2000
    end

    local growthFunctions = {
        MaxSize = sizeGrowth,
        Speed = speedGrowth,
        Multiplier = multiplierGrowth,
        EatSpeed = eatSpeedGrowth,
    }

    local priceFunctions = {
        MaxSize = sizePrice,
        Speed = speedPrice,
        Multiplier = multiplierPrice,
        EatSpeed = eatSpeedPrice,
    }

    -- 数字加千分位
    local function formatNumber(n)
        local s = tostring(math.floor(tonumber(n) or 0))
        local k
        repeat
            s, k = s:gsub("^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2")
        until k == 0
        return s
    end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "PlayerDataUI"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.DisplayOrder = 997
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    local main = Instance.new("Frame")
    main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(260, 260)
    main.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    main.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    main.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 32)
    main.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    main.BorderSizePixel = 0
    main.Parent = gui

    local mainCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    mainCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    mainCorner.Parent = main

    local mainStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    mainStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80)
    mainStroke.Thickness = 1
    mainStroke.Parent = main

    -- 标题栏
    local titleBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    titleBar.Name = "TitleBar"
    titleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    titleBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 46)
    titleBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    titleBar.Parent = main

    local titleCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    titleCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    titleCorner.Parent = titleBar

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 1, 0)
    title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    title.TextSize = 16
    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    title.Text = "Dữ liệu người chơi"
    title.Parent = titleBar

    local refreshBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    refreshBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 26)
    refreshBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -86, 0.5, -13)
    refreshBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
    refreshBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    refreshBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
    refreshBtn.TextSize = 14
    refreshBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    refreshBtn.Text = "Làm mới"
    refreshBtn.Parent = titleBar

    local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    closeBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
    closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0.5, -13)
    closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 60, 60)
    closeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    closeBtn.TextSize = 16
    closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    closeBtn.Text = "×"
    closeBtn.Parent = titleBar

    -- 列表
    local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    list.Name = "List"
    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -42)
    list.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 36)
    list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    list.BorderSizePixel = 0
    list.ScrollBarThickness = 6
    list.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 100)
    list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    list.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    list.Parent = main

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Parent = list

    local function createRow(order)
        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0)
        row.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 44)
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.LayoutOrder = order
        row.Parent = list

        local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        c.Parent = row

        local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
        pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)
        pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
        pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
        pad.Parent = row

        local inner = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        inner.Padding = UDim.new(0, 1)
        inner.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        inner.Parent = row

        return row
    end

    local function createLine(parent, order, text, bold, color)
        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        label.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Font = bold and Enum.Font.SourceSansBold or Enum.Font.SourceSans
        label.TextSize = bold and 14 or 12
        label.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 235)
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        label.TextWrapped = true
        label.Text = text
        label.LayoutOrder = order
        label.Parent = parent
        return label
    end

    local function render()
        for _, child in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Frame") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end

        local players = Players:GetPlayers()
        local order = 0

        if #players == 0 then
            order = order + 1
            local row = createRow(order)
            createLine(row, 1, "Không có người chơi", true)
        end

        for _, player in ipairs(players) do
            order = order + 1
            local row = createRow(order)

            local isMe = (player == LocalPlayer)
            local nameText = player.Name .. (isMe and " (Bạn)" or "")
            createLine(row, 1, nameText, true, Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 255))

            local upgrades = player:FindFirstChild("Upgrades")
            local count = 0

            if upgrades then
                for _, upg in ipairs(upgrades:GetChildren()) do
                    local cn = localization[upg.Name]
                    local growth = growthFunctions[upg.Name]
                    if cn and growth and typeof(upg.Value) == "number" then
                        count = count + 1
                        local level = upg.Value
                        createLine(row, count + 1, string.format(
                            "%s: Cấp %d | Giá trị %s",
                            cn, level, formatNumber(growth(level))))
                    end
                end
            end

            if count == 0 then
                createLine(row, 2, "Không có dữ liệu (máy chủ không đồng bộ)", false,
                    Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 170))
            end
        end
    end

    -- 连接管理
    local conns = {}
    local function bind(signal, fn)
        local c = signal:Connect(fn)
        table.insert(conns, c)
        return c
    end

    gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
            pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
        end
        conns = {}
    end)

    -- 拖动
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos

    bind(titleBar.InputBegan, function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = main.Position

            local conn
            conn = input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    bind(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            main.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end)

    bind(refreshBtn.MouseButton1Click, function()
        render()
    end)

    bind(closeBtn.MouseButton1Click, function()
        gui:Destroy()
        playerDataUI = nil
    end)

    bind(Players.PlayerAdded, function()
        task.delay(1, function()
            if gui.Parent then render() end
        end)
    end)

    bind(Players.PlayerRemoving, function()
        if gui.Parent then render() end
    end)

    render()
    return gui
end

local function togglePlayerDataUI()
    if playerDataUI and playerDataUI.Parent then
        playerDataUI:Destroy()
        playerDataUI = nil
        Message("Xem dữ liệu", "Đã đóng bảng dữ liệu", 2)
    else
        playerDataUI = buildPlayerDataUI()
        Message("Xem dữ liệu", "Đã mở bảng dữ liệu", 2)
    end
end

-- 打滑消除
local function toggleSlippageElimination(enabled)
    if enabled then
        -- 开启打滑消除
        if slippageConnection then
            slippageConnection:Disconnect()
            slippageConnection = nil
        end
        
        slippageEliminationEnabled = true
        slippageConnection = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not slippageEliminationEnabled then return end
            
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not character then return end
            
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoid or not rootPart then return end
            
            -- 获取玩家输入的移动方向
            local moveDirection = humanoid.MoveDirection
            
            -- 直接使用Humanoid当前的WalkSpeed作为最大速度
            local maxSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed
            
            -- 计算目标速度向量
            local targetVelocity = moveDirection * maxSpeed
            
            -- 使用线性插值平滑过渡速度
            if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
                -- 加速时：向目标速度靠近
                currentVelocity = currentVelocity:Lerp(targetVelocity, ACCELERATION)
            else
                -- 减速时：逐渐归零
                currentVelocity = currentVelocity:Lerp(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), DECELERATION)
            end
            
            -- 应用速度到角色（保留Y轴速度）
            local rootVelocity = rootPart.Velocity
            rootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(currentVelocity.X, rootVelocity.Y, currentVelocity.Z)
        end)
        Message("Chống trượt", "Đã bật chống trượt", 2)
    else
        -- 关闭打滑消除
        slippageEliminationEnabled = false
        if slippageConnection then
            slippageConnection:Disconnect()
            slippageConnection = nil
        end
        Message("Chống trượt", "Đã tắt chống trượt", 2)
    end
end


	-- ===================== GHIM NHÂN VẬT, GHIM TIÊU CỰ MÀN HÌNH & SHIFT LOCK =====================
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local pinCharacterEnabled = false
local pinCharacterConn = nil
local savedPinnedCFrame = nil
local savedWalkSpeed = nil
local savedJumpPower = nil
local savedUseJumpPower = nil

local pinCameraEnabled = false
local savedCameraCFrame = nil
local savedCameraFOV = nil
local savedCameraSubject = nil

local shiftLockEnabled = false
local shiftLockRenderConn = nil
local shiftLockCharConn = nil
local savedAutoRotate = nil
local SHIFT_LOCK_OFFSET = Vector3.new(1.75, 0.25, 0)
local SHIFT_LOCK_DISTANCE = 10

local hotkeyRConn = nil
local syncPinPanelButtons = nil
local pinPanelGui = nil
local pinPanelEnabled = false

local CHAR_BIND_NAME = "PinCharacterLock_Bind"
local CAM_BIND_NAME = "PinCameraLock_Bind"
local SHIFT_LOCK_BIND_NAME = "CustomShiftLock_Bind"

local function notify(title, text)
	if type(Message) == "function" then
		pcall(function()
			Message(title, text, 2)
		end)
	else
		print("[" .. tostring(title) .. "] " .. tostring(text))
	end
end

local function getLocalPlr()
	return LocalPlayer or Players.LocalPlayer
end

local function getGuiParent()
	local parent = nil
	pcall(function()
		if gethui then
			parent = gethui()
		end
	end)
	if not parent then
		pcall(function()
			parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
		end)
	end
	local lp = getLocalPlr()
	if not parent and lp then
		parent = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
	end
	return parent
end

local function freezeHumanoidMovement(humanoid)
	if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then return end
	pcall(function()
		if savedWalkSpeed == nil then
			savedWalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed
		end
		if savedUseJumpPower == nil then
			savedUseJumpPower = humanoid.UseJumpPower
		end
		if savedJumpPower == nil then
			savedJumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower
		end
		humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
		if humanoid.UseJumpPower then
			humanoid.JumpPower = 0
		end
	end)
end

local function restoreHumanoidMovement(humanoid)
	if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then return end
	pcall(function()
		if savedUseJumpPower ~= nil then
			humanoid.UseJumpPower = savedUseJumpPower
		end
		if savedWalkSpeed ~= nil then
			humanoid.WalkSpeed = savedWalkSpeed
		end
		if savedJumpPower ~= nil then
			humanoid.JumpPower = savedJumpPower
		end
	end)
end

-- Tính năng 1: Ghim nhân vật đứng yên tại vị trí hiện tại
local function togglePinCharacter(enabled)
	pcall(function()
		RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(CHAR_BIND_NAME)
	end)
	if pinCharacterConn then
		pinCharacterConn:Disconnect()
		pinCharacterConn = nil
	end

	local lp = getLocalPlr()
	local character = lp and lp.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local rootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if enabled then
		pinCharacterEnabled = true

		if rootPart then
			savedPinnedCFrame = rootPart.CFrame
			pcall(function()
				rootPart.Anchored = true
				rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				rootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
		end
		freezeHumanoidMovement(humanoid)

		pcall(function()
			RunService:BindToRenderStep(CHAR_BIND_NAME, Enum.RenderPriority.Input.Value + 1, function()
				if not pinCharacterEnabled then return end
				local curPlr = getLocalPlr()
				local char = curPlr and curPlr.Character
				local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if hrp then
					if not savedPinnedCFrame then
						savedPinnedCFrame = hrp.CFrame
					end
					pcall(function()
						hrp.Anchored = true
						hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
						if (hrp.Position - savedPinnedCFrame.Position).Magnitude > 0.01 or hrp.CFrame ~= savedPinnedCFrame then
							hrp.CFrame = savedPinnedCFrame
						end
					end)
				end
				freezeHumanoidMovement(hum)
			end)
		end)

		if lp then
			pinCharacterConn = lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
				if not pinCharacterEnabled then return end
				savedPinnedCFrame = nil
				savedWalkSpeed = nil
				savedJumpPower = nil
				savedUseJumpPower = nil
				local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
				local newHum = newChar:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
				if newHrp then
					savedPinnedCFrame = newHrp.CFrame
					pcall(function()
						newHrp.Anchored = true
					end)
				end
				freezeHumanoidMovement(newHum)
			end)
		end

		notify("Ghim", "Đã bật ghim nhân vật")
	else
		pinCharacterEnabled = false
		savedPinnedCFrame = nil

		if rootPart then
			pcall(function()
				rootPart.Anchored = false
				rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				rootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
		end
		restoreHumanoidMovement(humanoid)
		savedWalkSpeed = nil
		savedJumpPower = nil
		savedUseJumpPower = nil

		notify("Ghim", "Đã tắt ghim nhân vật")
	end

	if syncPinPanelButtons then
		syncPinPanelButtons()
	end
end

-- Tính năng 2: Ghim tiêu cự màn hình (cố định góc nhìn & tiêu cự camera tại lúc bật)
local function togglePinCamera(enabled)
	pcall(function()
		RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(CAM_BIND_NAME)
	end)

	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

	if enabled then
		pinCameraEnabled = true
		if camera then
			savedCameraCFrame = camera.CFrame
			savedCameraFOV = camera.FieldOfView
			savedCameraSubject = camera.CameraSubject
			pcall(function()
				camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
				camera.CFrame = savedCameraCFrame
				camera.FieldOfView = savedCameraFOV
			end)
		end

		pcall(function()
			RunService:BindToRenderStep(CAM_BIND_NAME, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 5, function()
				if not pinCameraEnabled then return end
				local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
				if cam and savedCameraCFrame then
					pcall(function()
						if cam.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
							cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
						end
						cam.CFrame = savedCameraCFrame
						if savedCameraFOV then
							cam.FieldOfView = savedCameraFOV
						end
					end)
				end
			end)
		end)

		notify("Ghim màn hình", "Đã bật ghim tiêu cự màn hình")
	else
		pinCameraEnabled = false
		if camera then
			pcall(function()
				camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
				if savedCameraSubject and savedCameraSubject.Parent then
					camera.CameraSubject = savedCameraSubject
				else
					local lp = getLocalPlr()
					local char = lp and lp.Character
					local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if hum then
						camera.CameraSubject = hum
					end
				end
				if savedCameraFOV then
					camera.FieldOfView = savedCameraFOV
				end
			end)
		end
		savedCameraCFrame = nil
		savedCameraFOV = nil
		savedCameraSubject = nil

		notify("Ghim màn hình", "Đã tắt ghim tiêu cự màn hình")
	end

	if syncPinPanelButtons then
		syncPinPanelButtons()
	end
end

-- Tính năng 3: Shift Lock (khóa chuột ở tâm màn hình, xoay góc nhìn theo chuột & xoay nhân vật theo hướng camera)
local function applyShiftLockState(character, active)
	if not character then return end
	local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if hum then
		pcall(function()
			if active then
				if savedAutoRotate == nil then
					savedAutoRotate = hum.AutoRotate
				end
				hum.AutoRotate = false
				hum.CameraOffset = SHIFT_LOCK_OFFSET
			else
				hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.zero
				if savedAutoRotate ~= nil then
					hum.AutoRotate = savedAutoRotate
				else
					hum.AutoRotate = true
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function toggleShiftLock(enabled)
	pcall(function()
		RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(SHIFT_LOCK_BIND_NAME)
	end)
	if shiftLockRenderConn then
		shiftLockRenderConn:Disconnect()
		shiftLockRenderConn = nil
	end
	if shiftLockCharConn then
		shiftLockCharConn:Disconnect()
		shiftLockCharConn = nil
	end

	local lp = getLocalPlr()
	local character = lp and lp.Character

	if enabled then
		shiftLockEnabled = true
		savedAutoRotate = nil
		applyShiftLockState(character, true)

		pcall(function()
			UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
		end)

		local function stepShiftLock()
			if not shiftLockEnabled or pinCameraEnabled then return end
			local curPlr = getLocalPlr()
			local curChar = curPlr and curPlr.Character
			local hrp = curChar and curChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local hum = curChar and curChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not hrp or not cam then return end

			pcall(function()
				UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
				if hum then
