					hum.AutoRotate = false
					hum.CameraOffset = SHIFT_LOCK_OFFSET
				end

				-- Giữ góc nhìn sau lưng nhân vật và xoay theo hướng camera hiện tại
				local lookDir = cam.CFrame.LookVector
				local flatLook = Vector3.new(lookDir.X, 0, lookDir.Z)
				if flatLook.Magnitude > 0.001 then
					flatLook = flatLook.Unit
					if not pinCharacterEnabled then
						hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + flatLook)
					end
				end

				if cam.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
					local focusPos = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.6, 0) + cam.CFrame.RightVector * SHIFT_LOCK_OFFSET.X
					local camPos = focusPos - lookDir.Unit * SHIFT_LOCK_DISTANCE
					cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camPos, focusPos)
				end
			end)
		end

		local boundOk = pcall(function()
			RunService:BindToRenderStep(SHIFT_LOCK_BIND_NAME, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2, stepShiftLock)
		end)
		if not boundOk then
			shiftLockRenderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(stepShiftLock)
		end

		if lp then
			shiftLockCharConn = lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
				if not shiftLockEnabled then return end
				savedAutoRotate = nil
				newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
				newChar:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
				applyShiftLockState(newChar, true)
			end)
		end

		notify("Shift Lock", "Đã bật Shift Lock")
	else
		shiftLockEnabled = false
		applyShiftLockState(character, false)
		savedAutoRotate = nil

		pcall(function()
			UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
			local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
			if cam and not pinCameraEnabled and cam.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
				cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			end
		end)

		notify("Shift Lock", "Đã tắt Shift Lock")
	end

	if syncPinPanelButtons then
		syncPinPanelButtons()
	end
end

-- Lệnh dừng nhanh bằng phím R trên PC/Laptop
local function stopAllPinModesByHotkey()
	local stoppedAny = false

	if pinCharacterEnabled then
		togglePinCharacter(false)
		stoppedAny = true
	end
	if pinCameraEnabled then
		togglePinCamera(false)
		stoppedAny = true
	end
	if shiftLockEnabled then
		toggleShiftLock(false)
		stoppedAny = true
	end

	if syncPinPanelButtons then
		syncPinPanelButtons()
	end

	if not stoppedAny then
		notify("Phím R", "Không có chế độ nào đang bật")
	end
end

if hotkeyRConn then
	hotkeyRConn:Disconnect()
	hotkeyRConn = nil
end

hotkeyRConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
	if gameProcessed then return end
	if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.R then
		stopAllPinModesByHotkey()
	end
end)

-- Ô tính năng nổi (Ghim nhân vật, Ghim tiêu cự & Shift Lock) có thể kéo di chuyển
local function createPinControlPanel()
	local parent = getGuiParent()
	if not parent then return end

	local oldGui = parent:FindFirstChild("PinControlPanelGui")
	if oldGui then
		oldGui:Destroy()
	end

	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	gui.Name = "PinControlPanelGui"
	gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
	gui.DisplayOrder = 950
	gui.Enabled = false
	gui.Parent = parent
	pinPanelGui = gui

	local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
	panel.Name = "PinPanel"
	panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 176, 0, 117)
	panel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, -58)
	panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 30, 30)
	panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
	panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
	panel.Parent = gui

	local panelCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	panelCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
	panelCorner.Parent = panel

	local panelStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	panelStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(95, 90, 90)
	panelStroke.Transparency = 0.35
	panelStroke.Thickness = 1
	panelStroke.Parent = panel

	local header = Instance.new("TextButton")
	header.Name = "DragHeader"
	header.Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 22)
	header.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 2)
	header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	header.Text = "Bảng Ghim [R: Dừng]"
	header.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	header.TextSize = 13
	header.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
	header.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	header.AutoButtonColor = false
	header.Parent = panel

	local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	closeBtn.Name = "ClosePanelBtn"
	closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
	closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 2)
	closeBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	closeBtn.Text = "×"
	closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	closeBtn.TextSize = 18
	closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 218)
	closeBtn.Parent = panel

	-- Kéo thả di chuyển bảng
	do
		local dragging = false
		local dragStart = nil
		local startPos = nil
		local renderConn = nil

		local function stopDrag()
			dragging = false
			dragStart = nil
			startPos = nil
			if renderConn then
				renderConn:Disconnect()
				renderConn = nil
			end
		end

		header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				dragging = true
				dragStart = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
				startPos = panel.Position

				if renderConn then
					renderConn:Disconnect()
				end
				renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
					if not dragging or not dragStart or not startPos or not panel.Parent then
						stopDrag()
						return
					end
					local delta = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation() - dragStart
					panel.Position = UDim2.new(
						startPos.X.Scale,
						startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
						startPos.Y.Scale,
						startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
					)
				end)

				input.Changed:Connect(function()
					if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
						stopDrag()
					end
				end)
			end
		end)

		UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				if dragging then
					stopDrag()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local charBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	charBtn.Name = "PinCharBtn"
	charBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 25)
	charBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 26)
	charBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 46, 46)
	charBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
	charBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	charBtn.Text = "Ghim nhân vật: Tắt"
	charBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	charBtn.TextSize = 12
	charBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
	charBtn.Parent = panel

	local charCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	charCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	charCorner.Parent = charBtn

	local camBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	camBtn.Name = "PinCamBtn"
	camBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 25)
	camBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 55)
	camBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 46, 46)
	camBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
	camBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	camBtn.Text = "Ghim tiêu cự: Tắt"
	camBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	camBtn.TextSize = 12
	camBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
	camBtn.Parent = panel

	local camCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	camCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	camCorner.Parent = camBtn

	local lockBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	lockBtn.Name = "ShiftLockBtn"
	lockBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 25)
	lockBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 84)
	lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 46, 46)
	lockBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
	lockBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	lockBtn.Text = "Shift Lock: Tắt"
	lockBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	lockBtn.TextSize = 12
	lockBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
	lockBtn.Parent = panel

	local lockCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	lockCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	lockCorner.Parent = lockBtn

	syncPinPanelButtons = function()
		if not panel or not panel.Parent then return end
		charBtn.Text = pinCharacterEnabled and "Ghim nhân vật: Bật" or "Ghim nhân vật: Tắt"
		charBtn.BackgroundColor3 = pinCharacterEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(75, 165, 95) or Color3.fromRGB(48, 46, 46)

		camBtn.Text = pinCameraEnabled and "Ghim tiêu cự: Bật" or "Ghim tiêu cự: Tắt"
		camBtn.BackgroundColor3 = pinCameraEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(75, 165, 95) or Color3.fromRGB(48, 46, 46)

		lockBtn.Text = shiftLockEnabled and "Shift Lock: Bật" or "Shift Lock: Tắt"
		lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = shiftLockEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(75, 165, 95) or Color3.fromRGB(48, 46, 46)
	end

	closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		pinPanelEnabled = false
		if pinPanelGui then
			pinPanelGui.Enabled = false
		end
	end)

	charBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		togglePinCharacter(not pinCharacterEnabled)
	end)

	camBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		togglePinCamera(not pinCameraEnabled)
	end)

	lockBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		toggleShiftLock(not shiftLockEnabled)
	end)

	syncPinPanelButtons()
end

-- Hàm bật/tắt bảng Ghim từ menu hoặc script chính
local function togglePinPanel(enabled)
	if type(enabled) == "boolean" then
		pinPanelEnabled = enabled
	else
		pinPanelEnabled = not pinPanelEnabled
	end

	if not pinPanelGui or not pinPanelGui.Parent then
		createPinControlPanel()
	end

	if pinPanelGui then
		pinPanelGui.Enabled = pinPanelEnabled
	end

	if syncPinPanelButtons then
		syncPinPanelButtons()
	end
end

local function TogglePinUI()
	togglePinPanel(not pinPanelEnabled)
end

createPinControlPanel()





-- 重力锁定
local function toggleGravityLock(enabled)
    if enabled then
        -- 开启重力锁定
        if gravityLockConnection then
            task.cancel(gravityLockConnection)
            gravityLockConnection = nil
        end
        
        gravityLockEnabled = true
        gravityLockConnection = task.spawn(function()
            while gravityLockEnabled do
                workspace.Gravity = 99999999 -- 默认重力
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end)
        Message("Gravity", "Đã bật Gravity", 2)
    else
        -- 关闭重力锁定
        gravityLockEnabled = false
        if gravityLockConnection then
            task.cancel(gravityLockConnection)
            gravityLockConnection = nil
        end
        Message("Gravity", "Đã tắt Gravity", 2)
    end
end

-- 抓取功能
local function grabForwardObject()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not character then return end
    
    local events = character:FindFirstChild("Events")
    if not events then return end
    
    local grabEvent = events:FindFirstChild("Grab")
    if not grabEvent then return end
    
    grabEvent:FireServer(false, false, true)
end

-- 创建持续抓取UI
local function CreateContinuousGrabUI()
    if continuousGrabUI then
        continuousGrabUI:Destroy()
        continuousGrabUI = nil
    end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "ContinuousGrabUI"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    gui.Enabled = true
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    pcall(function()
        gui.Parent = CoreGui
    end)
    if gui.Parent ~= CoreGui then
        gui.Parent = PlayerGui
    end

    local mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    mainFrame.Name = "mainFrame"
    mainFrame.Parent = gui
    mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28)
    mainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0.55, 0)
    mainFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 64)
    mainFrame.Active = true
    mainFrame.Draggable = false
    mainFrame.ClipsDescendants = true

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    corner.Parent = mainFrame

    local shadow = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    shadow.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 120)
    shadow.Transparency = 0
    shadow.Thickness = 2
    shadow.Parent = mainFrame

    local grabButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    grabButton.Name = "grabButton"
    grabButton.Parent = mainFrame
    grabButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34)
    grabButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    grabButton.AutoButtonColor = false
    grabButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
    grabButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    grabButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    grabButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    grabButton.Text = "✋"
    grabButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    grabButton.TextSize = 32
    grabButton.ZIndex = 2

    local buttonCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    buttonCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    buttonCorner.Parent = grabButton

    local dragging = false
    local dragStart = Vector2.new()
    local startPos = UDim2.new()
    local clickStarted = false
    local clickStartPos = Vector2.new()

    grabButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            clickStarted = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            clickStartPos = input.Position
            startPos = mainFrame.Position
            UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = false
        end
    end)

    grabButton.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale,
                startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale,
                startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end)

    grabButton.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
            UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
            
            if clickStarted then
                local delta = input.Position - clickStartPos
                if delta.Magnitude < 5 then
                    grabForwardObject()
                end
                clickStarted = false
            end
        end
    end)

    grabButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(grabButton, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 52)
        }):Play()
    end)

    grabButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(grabButton, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34)
        }):Play()
    end)

    continuousGrabUI = gui
    return gui
end

-- 持续抓取
local function toggleContinuousGrab(enabled)
    if enabled then
        continuousGrabEnabled = true
        CreateContinuousGrabUI()
        
        continuousGrabConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
            if processed then return end
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F then
                grabForwardObject()
            end
        end)
        
        Message("Tự động bắt", "Đã bật tự động bắt\nNhấn F để bắt", 2)
    else
        continuousGrabEnabled = false
        
        if continuousGrabConnection then
            continuousGrabConnection:Disconnect()
            continuousGrabConnection = nil
        end
        
        if continuousGrabUI then
            continuousGrabUI:Destroy()
            continuousGrabUI = nil
        end
        
        Message("Tự động bắt", "Đã tắt tự động bắt", 2)
    end
end

-- 获取玩家进食量
local function getCurrency()
    -- 尝试从不同位置获取进食量
    local currency = 0
    
    -- 方法1: 检查Leaderstats
    if LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("leaderstats") then
        local leaderstats = LocalPlayer.leaderstats
        -- 常见的货币名称
        local currencyNames = {"Money", "Cash", "Coins", "Gold", "Currency", "USD", "Xu", "Tiền", "Dollars", "Credits", "Points", "Tokens", "Gems"}
        
        for _, name in ipairs(currencyNames) do
            local currencyValue = leaderstats:FindFirstChild(name)
            if currencyValue and (currencyValue:IsA("IntValue") or currencyValue:IsA("NumberValue") or currencyValue:IsA("Int64Value")) then
                currency = currencyValue.Value
                return currency
            end
        end
        
        -- 检查leaderstats中的所有数值类型子对象
        for _, child in ipairs(leaderstats:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("IntValue") or child:IsA("NumberValue") or child:IsA("Int64Value") then
                currency = child.Value
                return currency
            end
        end
    end
    
    -- 方法2: 检查Currencies文件夹
    if LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Currencies") then
        local currencies = LocalPlayer.Currencies
        for _, currencyValue in ipairs(currencies:GetChildren()) do
            if currencyValue:IsA("IntValue") or currencyValue:IsA("NumberValue") or currencyValue:IsA("Int64Value") then
                currency = currencyValue.Value
                return currency
            end
        end
    end
    
    -- 方法3: 检查Player属性
    for _, child in ipairs(LocalPlayer:GetChildren()) do
        if (child:IsA("IntValue") or child:IsA("NumberValue") or child:IsA("Int64Value")) and 
           (string.lower(child.Name):find("money") or string.lower(child.Name):find("cash") or 
            string.lower(child.Name):find("coin") or string.lower(child.Name):find("gold") or
            string.lower(child.Name):find("currency") or string.lower(child.Name):find("dollar") or
            string.lower(child.Name):find("credit") or string.lower(child.Name):find("point") or
            string.lower(child.Name):find("token") or string.lower(child.Name):find("gem")) then
            currency = child.Value
            return currency
        end
    end
    
    -- 方法4: 检查可能的特殊存储位置
    local specialLocations = {
        LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Stats"),
        LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Data"),
        LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerData"),
        LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Currency")
    }
    
    for _, location in ipairs(specialLocations) do
        if location then
            for _, child in ipairs(location:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("IntValue") or child:IsA("NumberValue") or child:IsA("Int64Value") then
                    currency = child.Value
                    return currency
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    return currency
end

-- 根据进食量计算传送距离
local function getDistanceByCurrency(currency)
    if currency < 3000 then
        return 0
    elseif currency < 15000 then
        return 6
    elseif currency < 40000 then
        return 10
    elseif currency < 80000 then
        return 20
    elseif currency < 150000 then
        return 30
    elseif currency < 300000 then
        return 40
    elseif currency < 500000 then
        return 50
    elseif currency < 800000 then
        return 60
    elseif currency < 1200000 then
        return 70
    elseif currency < 2000000 then
        return 80
    elseif currency < 3000000 then
        return 90
    elseif currency < 4000000 then
        return 100
    elseif currency < 5500000 then
        return 110
    elseif currency < 6300000 then
        return 120
    elseif currency < 8000000 then
        return 130
    else
        return 210
    end
end

-- 传送抓人
-- 传送抓人
local function getForwardGrabPosition(character)
    local rootPart = character.HumanoidRootPart
    local direction = rootPart.CFrame.LookVector
    local distance = 30
    local grabPosition = rootPart.Position + direction * distance
    return grabPosition
end

local pinnedTeleportUsers = {} -- [UserId] = true/false (dùng chung cho cả Dịch chuyển bắt & Dịch chuyển ném)

local function makeFrameMovable(targetFrame, dragHandle)
    local dragging = false
    local dragStart = nil
    local startPos = nil
    local renderConn = nil

    local function stopDragging()
        dragging = false
        dragStart = nil
        startPos = nil
        if renderConn then
            renderConn:Disconnect()
            renderConn = nil
        end
    end

    dragHandle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
            startPos = targetFrame.Position

            if renderConn then
                renderConn:Disconnect()
            end

            renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not dragging or not dragStart or not startPos or not targetFrame.Parent then
                    stopDragging()
                    return
                end

                local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                local delta = mousePos - dragStart
                targetFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale,
                    startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale,
                    startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                )
            end)

            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    stopDragging()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if dragging then
                stopDragging()
            end
        end
    end)

    targetFrame.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
        if not parent then
            stopDragging()
        end
    end)
end

local function buildTeleportPlayerListUI(config)
    local ROW_HEIGHT = 35
    local ROW_PADDING = 5
    local MAX_NO_SCROLL_PLAYERS = 5
    local HEADER_HEIGHT = 40
    local listFrameHeight = (ROW_HEIGHT * MAX_NO_SCROLL_PLAYERS) + (ROW_PADDING * (MAX_NO_SCROLL_PLAYERS - 1))

    local darkBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(32, 30, 30)
    local headerBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(40, 38, 38)
    local rowBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(48, 46, 46)
    local rowHoverColor = Color3.fromRGB(62, 59, 59)
    local borderColor = Color3.fromRGB(95, 90, 90)
    local textLightColor = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
    local subTextColor = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 198)

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = config.guiName
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    gui.Enabled = true
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    pcall(function()
        gui.Parent = CoreGui
    end)
    if gui.Parent ~= CoreGui then
        gui.Parent = PlayerGui
    end

    local mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    mainFrame.Name = "mainFrame"
    mainFrame.Parent = gui
    mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = darkBgColor
    mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    mainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
    mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, HEADER_HEIGHT + listFrameHeight + 12)
    mainFrame.Active = true
    mainFrame.Draggable = false
    mainFrame.ClipsDescendants = true

    -- 窗口圆角
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    corner.Parent = mainFrame

    -- 窗口阴影 / 边框
    local shadow = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    shadow.Color = borderColor
    shadow.Transparency = 0.35
    shadow.Thickness = 1
    shadow.Parent = mainFrame

    local titleBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    titleBar.Name = "titleBar"
    titleBar.Parent = mainFrame
    titleBar.BackgroundColor3 = headerBgColor
    titleBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    titleBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    titleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, HEADER_HEIGHT)
    titleBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    titleBar.Active = true
    titleBar.Draggable = false

    -- Nút kéo trong suốt phủ toàn bộ thanh tiêu đề để nhận input ổn định trên mọi môi trường
    local dragButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    dragButton.Name = "dragButton"
    dragButton.Parent = titleBar
    dragButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    dragButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
    dragButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    dragButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
    dragButton.Text = ""
    dragButton.AutoButtonColor = false
    dragButton.ZIndex = 2

    local titleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    titleLabel.Name = "titleLabel"
    titleLabel.Parent = titleBar
    titleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
    titleLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    titleLabel.Text = config.titleText
    titleLabel.TextColor3 = textLightColor
    titleLabel.TextSize = 16
    titleLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    titleLabel.ZIndex = 3

    local closeButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    closeButton.Name = "closeButton"
    closeButton.Parent = titleBar
    closeButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    closeButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 0)
    closeButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 1, 0)
    closeButton.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    closeButton.Text = "×"
    closeButton.TextColor3 = textLightColor
    closeButton.TextSize = 20
    closeButton.ZIndex = 4
    closeButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        gui.Enabled = not gui.Enabled
    end)

    local playersScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    playersScroll.Name = "playersScroll"
    playersScroll.Parent = mainFrame
    playersScroll.Active = true
    playersScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    playersScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
    playersScroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, HEADER_HEIGHT + 6)
    playersScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, listFrameHeight)
    playersScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    playersScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 0
    playersScroll.ScrollingEnabled = false
    playersScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 168)

    local UIListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    UIListLayout.Parent = playersScroll
    UIListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    UIListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, ROW_PADDING)

    local refreshList

    local function createPlayerRow(parent, plr, layoutOrder)
        local isPinned = pinnedTeleportUsers[plr.UserId] == true

        local rowFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        rowFrame.Name = plr.Name
        rowFrame.Parent = parent
        rowFrame.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder
        rowFrame.BackgroundColor3 = rowBgColor
        rowFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        rowFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        rowFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, ROW_HEIGHT)

        local rowCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        rowCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        rowCorner.Parent = rowFrame

        -- Ô tích hình vuông ở đầu dòng để ghim người chơi lên đầu danh sách
        local pinBox = Instance.new("TextButton")
        pinBox.Name = "PinCheckbox"
        pinBox.Parent = rowFrame
        pinBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
        pinBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -9)
        pinBox.BackgroundColor3 = isPinned and Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 218) or Color3.fromRGB(36, 34, 34)
        pinBox.BackgroundTransparency = isPinned and 0.1 or 0.3
        pinBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        pinBox.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        pinBox.Text = isPinned and "✓" or ""
        pinBox.TextColor3 = isPinned and Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30) or textLightColor
        pinBox.TextSize = 13

        local pinCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        pinCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
        pinCorner.Parent = pinBox

        local pinStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        pinStroke.Color = isPinned and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240) or Color3.fromRGB(135, 132, 132)
        pinStroke.Thickness = 1
        pinStroke.Parent = pinBox

        pinBox.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            pinnedTeleportUsers[plr.UserId] = not (pinnedTeleportUsers[plr.UserId] == true)
            if refreshList then
                refreshList()
            end
        end)

        -- Nút chọn người chơi để thực hiện hành động
        local playerbutton = Instance.new("TextButton")
        playerbutton.Name = "ActionButton"
        playerbutton.Parent = rowFrame
        playerbutton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        playerbutton.BorderSizePixel = 0
        playerbutton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 0)
        playerbutton.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0)
        playerbutton.Font = isPinned and Enum.Font.SourceSansBold or Enum.Font.SourceSans
        local displayLabel = plr.Name
        if plr.Name ~= plr.DisplayName then
            displayLabel = displayLabel .. " (" .. plr.DisplayName .. ")"
        end
        if isPinned then
            displayLabel = "★ " .. displayLabel
        end
        playerbutton.Text = displayLabel
        playerbutton.TextColor3 = isPinned and textLightColor or subTextColor
        playerbutton.TextSize = 14
        playerbutton.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        playerbutton.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
        playerbutton.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

        playerbutton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            config.onPlayerAction(plr)
        end)

        -- 按钮悬停效果
        playerbutton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(rowFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
                BackgroundColor3 = rowHoverColor
            }):Play()
        end)

        playerbutton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(rowFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
                BackgroundColor3 = rowBgColor
            }):Play()
        end)
    end

    refreshList = function()
        if not gui or not gui.Parent or not playersScroll or not playersScroll.Parent then
            return
        end

        for _, child in ipairs(playersScroll:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Frame") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end

        local allPlayers = Players:GetPlayers()
        table.sort(allPlayers, function(a, b)
            local aPinned = pinnedTeleportUsers[a.UserId] == true
            local bPinned = pinnedTeleportUsers[b.UserId] == true
            if aPinned ~= bPinned then
                return aPinned -- Người được ghim xếp lên đầu danh sách
            end
            return a.Name:lower() < b.Name:lower()
        end)

        for idx, plr in ipairs(allPlayers) do
            createPlayerRow(playersScroll, plr, idx)
        end

        local playerCount = #allPlayers
        local hasScroll = playerCount >= MAX_NO_SCROLL_PLAYERS
        local contentHeight = (playerCount > 0)
            and ((ROW_HEIGHT * playerCount) + (ROW_PADDING * (playerCount - 1)))
            or 0

        playersScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, contentHeight)
        playersScroll.ScrollingEnabled = hasScroll
        playersScroll.ScrollBarThickness = hasScroll and 4 or 0
    end

    refreshList()

    local addedConn = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
        if gui and gui.Parent then
            refreshList()
        end
    end)

    local removingConn = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
        pinnedTeleportUsers[plr.UserId] = nil
        if gui and gui.Parent then
            refreshList()
        end
    end)

    gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        if addedConn then addedConn:Disconnect() end
        if removingConn then removingConn:Disconnect() end
    end)

    -- Kéo thanh tiêu đề qua dragButton để di chuyển bảng
    makeFrameMovable(mainFrame, dragButton)

    -- 确保标题栏位置固定
    titleBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    titleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, HEADER_HEIGHT)

    return gui
end

local function CreateTeleportGrabUI()
    if teleportGrabUI then
        teleportGrabUI:Destroy()
        teleportGrabUI = nil
    end

    local gui = buildTeleportPlayerListUI({
        guiName = "PlayerTeleportTool",
        titleText = "Dịch chuyển bắt",
        onPlayerAction = function(plr)
            local localPlayer = LocalPlayer
            if not localPlayer or not localPlayer.Character then return end

            local localHumanoidRootPart = localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not localHumanoidRootPart then return end

            -- 先执行抓取命令（检查角色状态）
            if character and character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                grabForwardObject()
                task.wait(0.2) -- 短暂延迟，确保抓取命令执行
            end

            if plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                -- 计算玩家身后的位置，使用目标玩家的Y轴
                local playerRootPart = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                local currency = getCurrency()
                local distance = getDistanceByCurrency(currency)
                local behindPosition = playerRootPart.Position - playerRootPart.CFrame.LookVector * distance
                -- 改为使用目标玩家的Y轴
                behindPosition = Vector3.new(behindPosition.X, playerRootPart.Position.Y, behindPosition.Z)

                -- 传送到玩家身后，确保人物保持直立
                local direction = (playerRootPart.Position - behindPosition).Unit
                localHumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(behindPosition, behindPosition + Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z))
            end
        end
    })

    teleportGrabUI = gui
    return gui
end

local function ToggleTeleportGrabUI()
    if not teleportGrabUI then
        CreateTeleportGrabUI()
    else
        teleportGrabUI:Destroy()
        teleportGrabUI = nil
    end
end

-- 传送砸人
local function CreateTeleportThrowUI()
    if teleportThrowUI then
        teleportThrowUI:Destroy()
        teleportThrowUI = nil
    end

    local gui = buildTeleportPlayerListUI({
        guiName = "TeleportThrowTool",
        titleText = "Dịch chuyển ném",
        onPlayerAction = function(plr)
            local localPlayer = LocalPlayer
            if not localPlayer or not localPlayer.Character then return end

            local localHumanoidRootPart = localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not localHumanoidRootPart then return end

            -- 先执行抓取命令
            grabForwardObject()
            task.wait(0.4)

            if plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                -- 计算玩家身后的位置，保持自身Y轴不变
                local playerRootPart = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                local currency = getCurrency()
                local distance = getDistanceByCurrency(currency)
                local behindPosition = playerRootPart.Position - playerRootPart.CFrame.LookVector * distance
                behindPosition = Vector3.new(behindPosition.X, localHumanoidRootPart.Position.Y, behindPosition.Z)

                -- 传送到玩家身后，确保人物保持直立
                local direction = (playerRootPart.Position - behindPosition).Unit
                localHumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(behindPosition, behindPosition + Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z))
            end
        end
    })

    teleportThrowUI = gui
    return gui
end

local function ToggleTeleportThrowUI()
    if not teleportThrowUI then
        CreateTeleportThrowUI()
    else
        teleportThrowUI:Destroy()
        teleportThrowUI = nil
    end
end
-- 秒杀功能：请求布娃娃后立即起身（每点一次执行一次）
local function miaoShaAction()
    local ragdollRE = Events:FindFirstChild("Ragdoll")
    local unRagdollRE = Events:FindFirstChild("unRagdoll")
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if not (ragdollRE and unRagdollRE and char and hum) then
        Message("Kill", "Remote hoặc nhân vật không dùng được", 2)
        return
    end

    ragdollRE:FireServer()

    task.spawn(function()
        -- 等自己被布娃娃化（最多 1 秒）
        local t0 = tick()
        while tick() - t0 < 1 do
            if not char.Parent or not hum.Parent then return end
            local state = hum:GetState()
            if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll then
                break
            end
            task.wait(0.01)
        end

        if char.Parent and hum.Parent then
            unRagdollRE:FireServer(char)
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        end
    end)
end

-- 秒杀 UI：可拖动圆钮，由人物菜单的「秒杀」开关控制显示 / 隐藏
local miaoShaUI = nil
local miaoShaConns = {}
local miaoShaMoved = false

local function destroyMiaoShaUI()
    for _, c in ipairs(miaoShaConns) do
        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
    end
    miaoShaConns = {}
    if miaoShaUI then
        miaoShaUI:Destroy()
        miaoShaUI = nil
    end
end

local function createMiaoShaUI()
    if miaoShaUI then return end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "MiaoShaUI"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.DisplayOrder = 999
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = "MiaoShaButton"
    btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 64)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0.45, 0)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34)
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.Text = "☠"
    btn.TextSize = 32
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.Active = true
    btn.Parent = gui

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    corner.Parent = btn

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(235, 70, 70)
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Parent = btn

    -- 点击执行一次秒杀（拖动不算点击）
    table.insert(miaoShaConns, btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if miaoShaMoved then
            miaoShaMoved = false
            return
        end
        miaoShaAction()
    end))

    -- 电脑端 G 键，按一次执行一次
    table.insert(miaoShaConns, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
        if processed then return end
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.G then
            miaoShaAction()
        end
    end))

    -- 拖动逻辑
    local dragging = false
    local dragStart, startPos

    table.insert(miaoShaConns, btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            miaoShaMoved = false
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = btn.Position

            local conn
            conn = input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end
    end))

    table.insert(miaoShaConns, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
            if math.abs(delta.X) > 6 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 6 then
                miaoShaMoved = true
            end
        end
    end))

    miaoShaUI = gui
end

local function ToggleMiaoShaUI()
    if miaoShaUI then
        destroyMiaoShaUI()
        Message("Kill", "Đã đóng giao diện đứng dậy", 2)
    else
        createMiaoShaUI()
        Message("Kill", "Đã mở giao diện đứng dậy", 2)
    end
end

-- 透视功能 (Highlight viền trắng, ruột đỏ nhạt + Name/DisplayName đỏ + Bảng chọn người chơi)
local espSelectAll = true
local espSelectedUsers = {} -- [UserId] = true/false
local espSelectorGui = nil
local refreshESPSelectorList = nil
local espSelectorCollapsed = false

local function isPlayerSelectedForESP(player)
    if not player or player == Players.LocalPlayer then
        return false
    end
    if espSelectAll then
        return true
    end
    return espSelectedUsers[player.UserId] == true
end

local function cleanupESPForPlayer(player)
    if not player or not playerInfos[player.UserId] then return end

    local info = playerInfos[player.UserId]
    if info.billboardGui and info.billboardGui.Parent then
        info.billboardGui:Destroy()
    end

    if info.highlight and info.highlight.Parent then
        info.highlight:Destroy()
    end

    -- Dọn dẹp dự phòng nếu còn sót trên character
    if info.character then
        local oldHighlight = info.character:FindFirstChild("ESPHighlight")
        if oldHighlight then
            oldHighlight:Destroy()
        end
        for _, part in pairs(info.character:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                for _, child in pairs(part:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name == "ESPSurface" then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    playerInfos[player.UserId] = nil
end

local function createESPForPlayer(player)
    if not player or player == Players.LocalPlayer then return end
    if not isPlayerSelectedForESP(player) then
        cleanupESPForPlayer(player)
        return
    end

    local character = player.Character
    if not character then return end

    -- 已创建过且角色未变，直接跳过（避免重复叠加）
    local oldInfo = playerInfos[player.UserId]
    if oldInfo and oldInfo.character == character
        and oldInfo.billboardGui and oldInfo.billboardGui.Parent
        and oldInfo.highlight and oldInfo.highlight.Parent then
        return
    end

    -- 角色刚生成或刚死亡时 Head 可能尚未复制到客户端，交给 updateESP 下一帧重试
    local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then return end

    -- Dọn bản cũ nếu có trước khi tạo mới
    cleanupESPForPlayer(player)

    -- Tạo Highlight: viền trắng xung quanh, bên trong tô màu đỏ nhạt
    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.Name = "ESPHighlight"
    highlight.Adornee = character
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 110) -- Đỏ nhạt bên trong
    highlight.FillTransparency = 0.55
    highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) -- Viền trắng xung quanh
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
    highlight.Parent = character

    -- 创建名称标签 (Name và DisplayName đều tô màu đỏ)
    local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboardGui.Name = "ESPNameTag"
    billboardGui.Parent = head
    billboardGui.Adornee = head
    billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 38)
    billboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
    billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true

    local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    nameLabel.Parent = billboardGui
    nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 19)
    nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    nameLabel.Text = player.Name
    nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60) -- Màu đỏ
    nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
    nameLabel.TextSize = 14
    nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center

    local displayNameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    displayNameLabel.Parent = billboardGui
    displayNameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    displayNameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 19)
    displayNameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 19)
    displayNameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    displayNameLabel.Text = player.DisplayName
    displayNameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60) -- Màu đỏ
    displayNameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
    displayNameLabel.TextSize = 12
    displayNameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center

    playerInfos[player.UserId] = {
        character = character,
        billboardGui = billboardGui,
        highlight = highlight
    }
end

local function applyESPSelectionNow()
    if not espEnabled then return end
    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
            if isPlayerSelectedForESP(player) then
                createESPForPlayer(player)
            else
                cleanupESPForPlayer(player)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function destroyESPSelectorUI()
    if espSelectorGui and espSelectorGui.Parent then
        espSelectorGui:Destroy()
    end
    espSelectorGui = nil
    refreshESPSelectorList = nil
end

local function createESPSelectorUI()
    destroyESPSelectorUI()

    local old = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("ESPPlayerSelectorGui")
    if old then old:Destroy() end

    local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    screenGui.Name = "ESPPlayerSelectorGui"
    screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    screenGui.Parent = CoreGui
    espSelectorGui = screenGui

    local ROW_HEIGHT = 24
    local MAX_VISIBLE_ROWS = 4 -- Khi có 4 người chơi trở lên (tổng >= 5 dòng gồm All) sẽ bật thanh cuộn
    local HEADER_HEIGHT = 26
    local PADDING = 6
    local listMaxHeight = ROW_HEIGHT * MAX_VISIBLE_ROWS
    local expandedHeight = HEADER_HEIGHT + listMaxHeight + PADDING * 2
    local collapsedHeight = HEADER_HEIGHT + 4

    local darkBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(32, 30, 30)
    local borderColor = Color3.fromRGB(95, 90, 90)
    local dividerColor = Color3.fromRGB(115, 110, 110)
    local textLightColor = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
    local subTextColor = Color3.fromRGB(185, 185, 192)

    local mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    mainFrame.Name = "ESPSelectorFrame"
    mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 215, 0, espSelectorCollapsed and collapsedHeight or expandedHeight)
    mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, -80)
    mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = darkBgColor
    mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    mainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    mainFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    mainFrame.Parent = screenGui

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    corner.Parent = mainFrame

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = borderColor
    stroke.Transparency = 0.35
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    stroke.Parent = mainFrame

    -- Thanh tiêu đề (kéo để di chuyển bảng)
    local headerBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    headerBar.Name = "HeaderBar"
    headerBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, HEADER_HEIGHT)
    headerBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 2)
    headerBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    headerBar.Parent = mainFrame

    local titleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    titleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, -1)
    titleLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    titleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleLabel.Text = "ESP - Chọn Người Chơi"
    titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    titleLabel.TextSize = 13
    titleLabel.TextColor3 = textLightColor
    titleLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    titleLabel.Parent = headerBar

    -- Nút thu gọn / mở rộng bằng dấu gạch "-"
    local collapseBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    collapseBtn.Name = "CollapseButton"
    collapseBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
    collapseBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -10)
    collapseBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 48, 48)
    collapseBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    collapseBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    collapseBtn.Text = espSelectorCollapsed and "+" or "-"
    collapseBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    collapseBtn.TextSize = 16
    collapseBtn.TextColor3 = textLightColor
    collapseBtn.ZIndex = 3
    collapseBtn.Parent = headerBar

    local collapseCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    collapseCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    collapseCorner.Parent = collapseBtn

    local headerDivider = Instance.new("Frame")
    headerDivider.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
    headerDivider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1)
    headerDivider.BackgroundColor3 = dividerColor
    headerDivider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    headerDivider.BorderSizePixel = 0
    headerDivider.Parent = headerBar

    -- Kéo di chuyển bảng
    do
        local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos
        headerBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = mainFrame.Position
                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        dragging = false
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        headerBar.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragInput = input
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and input == dragInput then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale,
                    startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale,
                    startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                )
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Khung danh sách có thanh cuộn
    local scrollFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    scrollFrame.Name = "PlayerListScroll"
    scrollFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, listMaxHeight)
    scrollFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, HEADER_HEIGHT + 4)
    scrollFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    scrollFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scrollFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    scrollFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
    scrollFrame.ScrollingEnabled = false
    scrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    scrollFrame.Visible = not espSelectorCollapsed
    scrollFrame.Parent = mainFrame

    collapseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        espSelectorCollapsed = not espSelectorCollapsed
        scrollFrame.Visible = not espSelectorCollapsed
        headerDivider.Visible = not espSelectorCollapsed
        collapseBtn.Text = espSelectorCollapsed and "+" or "-"
        mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 215, 0, espSelectorCollapsed and collapsedHeight or expandedHeight)
    end)

    headerDivider.Visible = not espSelectorCollapsed

    local listLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    listLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    listLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 0)
    listLayout.Parent = scrollFrame

    local function createOptionRow(order, labelText, subText, isChecked, onClick)
        local rowBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        rowBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, ROW_HEIGHT)
        rowBtn.LayoutOrder = order
        rowBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 48, 48)
        rowBtn.BackgroundTransparency = isChecked and 0.45 or 0.85
        rowBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        rowBtn.Text = ""
        rowBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        rowBtn.Parent = scrollFrame

        -- Ô checkbox
        local checkBox = Instance.new("Frame")
        checkBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
        checkBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -7)
        checkBox.BackgroundColor3 = isChecked and Color3.fromRGB(220, 70, 70) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 43, 43)
        checkBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        checkBox.Parent = rowBtn

        local cbCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        cbCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
        cbCorner.Parent = checkBox

        local cbStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        cbStroke.Color = isChecked and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 135)
        cbStroke.Thickness = 1
        cbStroke.Parent = checkBox

        local checkMark = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        checkMark.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        checkMark.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        checkMark.Text = isChecked and "✓" or ""
        checkMark.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        checkMark.TextSize = 12
        checkMark.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        checkMark.Parent = checkBox

        local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -30, 1, -1)
        nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 0)
        nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLbl.Text = subText and (labelText .. " (" .. subText .. ")") or labelText
        nameLbl.Font = isChecked and Enum.Font.SourceSansBold or Enum.Font.SourceSans
        nameLbl.TextSize = 12
        nameLbl.TextColor3 = isChecked and textLightColor or subTextColor
        nameLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        nameLbl.Parent = rowBtn

        local divider = Instance.new("Frame")
        divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
        divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1)
        divider.BackgroundColor3 = dividerColor
        divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
        divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
        divider.Parent = rowBtn

        rowBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(onClick)
    end

    refreshESPSelectorList = function()
        if not scrollFrame or not scrollFrame.Parent then return end

        for _, child in ipairs(scrollFrame:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("TextButton") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end

        local otherPlayers = {}
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
                table.insert(otherPlayers, plr)
            end
        end
        table.sort(otherPlayers, function(a, b)
            return a.Name:lower() < b.Name:lower()
        end)

        -- Dòng 1: Chọn "All"
        createOptionRow(1, "All (Tất cả người chơi)", nil, espSelectAll, function()
            espSelectAll = not espSelectAll
            if espSelectAll then
                for _, plr in ipairs(otherPlayers) do
                    espSelectedUsers[plr.UserId] = true
                end
            else
                espSelectedUsers = {}
            end
            refreshESPSelectorList()
            applyESPSelectionNow()
        end)

        -- Các dòng người chơi
        for i, plr in ipairs(otherPlayers) do
            local checked = espSelectAll or (espSelectedUsers[plr.UserId] == true)
            createOptionRow(i + 1, plr.DisplayName, plr.Name, checked, function()
                if espSelectAll then
                    -- Đang bật All mà bấm bỏ chọn 1 người -> chuyển sang chọn từng người và bỏ người vừa bấm
                    espSelectAll = false
                    espSelectedUsers = {}
                    for _, p in ipairs(otherPlayers) do
                        if p.UserId ~= plr.UserId then
                            espSelectedUsers[p.UserId] = true
                        end
                    end
                else
                    espSelectedUsers[plr.UserId] = not espSelectedUsers[plr.UserId]
                    -- Nếu tất cả người chơi đều đã được tích -> tự động bật lại All
                    local allChecked = (#otherPlayers > 0)
                    for _, p in ipairs(otherPlayers) do
                        if not espSelectedUsers[p.UserId] then
                            allChecked = false
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    if allChecked then
                        espSelectAll = true
                    end
                end
                refreshESPSelectorList()
                applyESPSelectionNow()
            end)
        end

        -- Khi có 4 người chơi trở lên (tức >= 4 player + 1 dòng All = 5 dòng), kích hoạt thanh cuộn
        local playerCount = #otherPlayers
        local totalRows = playerCount + 1
        scrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, totalRows * ROW_HEIGHT)
        scrollFrame.ScrollingEnabled = (playerCount >= 4)
    end

    refreshESPSelectorList()
end

local function updateESP()
    if not espEnabled then return end

    -- 更新现有玩家
    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
            if isPlayerSelectedForESP(player) and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                local oldInfo = playerInfos[player.UserId]
                if not oldInfo or oldInfo.character ~= player.Character
                    or not oldInfo.highlight or not oldInfo.highlight.Parent
                    or not oldInfo.billboardGui or not oldInfo.billboardGui.Parent then
                    createESPForPlayer(player)
                end
            else
                cleanupESPForPlayer(player)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function startESP()
    -- 清理旧连接
    for _, connection in pairs(espConnections) do
        connection:Disconnect()
    end
    espConnections = {}

    -- Hiển thị bảng chọn người chơi
    createESPSelectorUI()

    -- 初始创建所有已选玩家的ESP
    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= Players.LocalPlayer and isPlayerSelectedForESP(player) then
            createESPForPlayer(player)
        end
    end

    -- 监听玩家加入
    table.insert(espConnections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
        if player ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
            if espSelectAll then
                espSelectedUsers[player.UserId] = true
            end
            if refreshESPSelectorList then
                refreshESPSelectorList()
            end
            player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                if isPlayerSelectedForESP(player) then
                    createESPForPlayer(player)
                end
            end)
        end
    end))

    -- 监听玩家离开
    table.insert(espConnections, Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
        cleanupESPForPlayer(player)
        espSelectedUsers[player.UserId] = nil
        if refreshESPSelectorList then
            refreshESPSelectorList()
        end
    end))

    -- 监听角色重生
    table.insert(espConnections, Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        -- 本地玩家重生时重新创建所有已选ESP
        for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
                cleanupESPForPlayer(player)
                if isPlayerSelectedForESP(player) then
                    createESPForPlayer(player)
                end
            end
        end
    end))

    -- 每帧更新
    table.insert(espConnections, game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(updateESP))

    print("ESP đã bật")
end

local function stopESP()
    -- 清理所有连接
    for _, connection in pairs(espConnections) do
        connection:Disconnect()
    end
    espConnections = {}

    -- 清理所有ESP
    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
            cleanupESPForPlayer(player)
        end
    end

    -- Đóng bảng chọn người chơi
    destroyESPSelectorUI()

    print("ESP đã tắt")
end

local function ToggleESP()
    espEnabled = not espEnabled
    if espEnabled then
        startESP()
        Message("ESP", "Đã bật ESP", 2)
    else
        stopESP()
        Message("ESP", "Đã tắt ESP", 2)
    end
end-- ===================== UI初始化 =====================
local mainUI = UI:NewWindow("Eat the World")

-- 自动子菜单
local autoSection = mainUI:NewSection("Tự động")
autoSection:CreateToggle("Auto Farm", function(enabled)
    autofarm = enabled
    config.autofarm = enabled
    autoSaveConfig()
    
    coroutine.wrap(function()
        local text = Drawing.new("Text")
        text.Outline = true
        text.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        text.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        text.Center = false
        text.Position = Vector2.new(64, 64)
        text.Text = ""
        text.Size = 14
        text.Visible = true
        
        local startTime = tick()
        local eatTime = 0
        local lastEatTime = tick()
        
        local timer = 0
        local grabTimer = 0
        local sellDebounce = false
        local sellCount = 0
        
        local bedrock = Instance.new("Part")
        bedrock.Anchored = true
        bedrock.Size = Vector3.new(2048, 10, 2048)
        bedrock.Position = Vector3.new(0, -5, 0)
        bedrock.BrickColor = BrickColor.Black()
        bedrock.Parent = workspace

        local map, chunks = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map"), workspace:FindFirstChild("Chunks")
        if map and chunks then
            map.Parent, chunks.Parent = nil, nil
        end

        local numChunks = 0
        
        local hum,
            root,
            size,
            events,
            eat,
            grab,
            sell,
            sendTrack,
            chunk,
            radius,
            autoConn,
            sizeConn
        
        local function onCharAdd(char)
            numChunks = 0
            
            hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
            root = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            size = char:WaitForChild("Size")
            events = char:WaitForChild("Events")
            eat = events:WaitForChild("Eat")
            grab = events:WaitForChild("Grab")
            sell = events:WaitForChild("Sell")
            chunk = char:WaitForChild("CurrentChunk")
            sendTrack = char:WaitForChild("SendTrack")
            radius = char:WaitForChild("Radius")
            autoConn = game["Run Service"].Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                if not autofarm then
                    autoConn:Disconnect()
                    return
                end
                
                local ran = tick() - startTime
                local hours = math.floor(ran / 60 / 60)
                local minutes = math.floor(ran / 60)
                local seconds = math.floor(ran)
                
                local eatMinutes = math.floor(eatTime / 60)
                local eatSeconds = math.floor(eatTime)
                
                local y = bedrock.Position.Y + bedrock.Size.Y / 2 + hum.HipHeight + root.Size.Y / 2

                local sizeAdd = LocalPlayer.Upgrades.Multiplier.Value / 100
                local addAmount = LocalPlayer.Upgrades.MaxSize.Value / sizeAdd
                
                local sellTime = addAmount / 2
                local sellMinutes = math.floor(sellTime / 60)
                local sellSeconds = math.floor(sellTime)
                
                local secondEarn = math.floor(((LocalPlayer.Upgrades.MaxSize.Value + 0.5) ^ 2 - 0.25) / 2 * 100 / sellTime)
                local minuteEarn = secondEarn * 60
                local hourEarn = minuteEarn * 60
                local dayEarn = hourEarn * 24
                
                text.Text = ""
                    .. "\nThời gian chạy: " .. string.format("%ih%im%is", hours, minutes % 60, seconds % 60)
                    .. "\nThời gian thực: " .. string.format("%im%is", eatMinutes % 60, eatSeconds % 60)
                    .. "\nThời gian ước tính: " .. string.format("%im%is", sellMinutes % 60, sellSeconds % 60)
                    .. "\nMỗi ngày: " .. dayEarn
                    .. "\nSố khối: " .. numChunks
                
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
                grab:FireServer()
                root.Anchored = false
                eat:FireServer()
                sendTrack:FireServer()
                
                if chunk.Value then
                    if timer > 0 then
                        numChunks += 1
                    end
                    timer = 0
                    grabTimer += dt
                else
                    timer += dt
                    grabTimer = 0
                end
                
                if timer > 60 then
                    hum.Health = 0
                    timer = 0
                    numChunks = 0
                end
                
                if grabTimer > 15 then
                    size.Value = LocalPlayer.Upgrades.MaxSize.Value
                end
                
                if (size.Value >= LocalPlayer.Upgrades.MaxSize.Value)
                    or timer > 8
                then
                    if timer < 8 then
                        sell:FireServer()
                        
                        if not sellDebounce then
                            local args = {
                                {
                                    MapTime = -1,
                                    Paused = true
                                }
                            }
                            Events.SetServerSettings:FireServer(unpack(args))
                        end
                        
                        sellDebounce = true
                    else
                        local args = {
                            {
                                MapTime = -1,
                                Paused = true
                            }
                        }
                        Events.SetServerSettings:FireServer(unpack(args))
                    end
                    numChunks = 0
                elseif size.Value == 0 then
                    if sellDebounce then
                        local currentEatTime = tick()
                        eatTime = currentEatTime - lastEatTime
                        lastEatTime = currentEatTime
                        
                        sellCount += 1
                    end
                    sellDebounce = false
                end
                
                if farmMoving then
                    local bound = 300
                    local startPos = CFrame.new(-bound/2, y, -bound/2)
                    
                    local r = radius.Value * 1.1
                    local dist = (r * numChunks)
                    local x = dist % bound
                    local z = math.floor(dist / bound) * r
                    local offset = CFrame.new(x, 0, z + r * 2)
                    
                    if z > bound then
                        local args = {
                            {
                                MapTime = -1,
                                Paused = true
                            }
                        }
                        Events.SetServerSettings:FireServer(unpack(args))
                        numChunks = 0
                    end
                    
                    root.CFrame = startPos * offset
                else
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, y, 0)
                end
            end)
            
            hum.Died:Connect(function()
                autoConn:Disconnect()
                local args = {
                    {
                        MapTime = -1,
                        Paused = true
                    }
                }
                Events.SetServerSettings:FireServer(unpack(args))
            end)
            
            char:WaitForChild("LocalChunkManager").Enabled = false
            char:WaitForChild("Animate").Enabled = false
        end
        
        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            task.spawn(onCharAdd, LocalPlayer.Character)
        else
            task.spawn(onCharAdd, LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait())
        end
        local charAddConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(onCharAdd)
        while autofarm do
            local dt = task.wait()
            if workspace:FindFirstChild("Loading") then
                workspace.Loading:Destroy()
            end
            if map and chunks then
                if showMap then
                    map.Parent, chunks.Parent = workspace, workspace
                else
                    map.Parent, chunks.Parent = nil, nil
                end
            end
        end
        charAddConn:Disconnect()
        if autoConn then
            autoConn:Disconnect()
        end
        if map and chunks then
            map.Parent, chunks.Parent = workspace, workspace
        end
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        bedrock:Destroy()
        LocalPlayer.Character.LocalChunkManager.Enabled = true
        LocalPlayer.Character.Animate.Enabled = true
        text:Destroy()
    end)()
end)
autoSection:CreateToggle("Receive", function(enabled)
    autoClaimRewards = enabled
    config.autoClaimRewards = enabled
    autoSaveConfig()
    
    coroutine.wrap(function()
        while autoClaimRewards do
            task.wait(1)
            for _, reward in LocalPlayer.TimedRewards:GetChildren() do
                if reward.Value > 0 then
                    Events.RewardEvent:FireServer(reward)
                end
            end
            
            Events.SpinEvent:FireServer()
        end
    end)()
end)
autoSection:CreateToggle("ShowMap", function(enabled)
    showMap = enabled
    config.showMap = enabled
    autoSaveConfig()
end)
autoSection:CreateToggle("Auto Eat", function(enabled)
    autoeat = enabled
    config.autoeat = enabled
    autoSaveConfig()
    
    coroutine.wrap(function()
        while autoeat do
            task.wait()
            
            if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Size") and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Events") and LocalPlayer.Character.Events:FindFirstChild("Grab") and LocalPlayer.Character.Events:FindFirstChild("Eat") and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("CurrentChunk") then
                LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = false
                LocalPlayer.Character.Events.Grab:FireServer()
                LocalPlayer.Character.Events.Eat:FireServer()
            end
        end
    end)()
end)
-- Tính năng "An toàn" (Bật/tắt tạo nền Platform tại Y = 4000 màu xám trong suốt + bảng nhỏ có nút ❤️ và 😜)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local platformBedrock = nil
local platformEnabled = (config and config.platformEnabled) or false
local PLATFORM_Y = 4000
local PLATFORM_HEIGHT = 2
local PLATFORM_SURFACE_Y = PLATFORM_Y + (PLATFORM_HEIGHT * 0.5) + 3.2

local safePlatformGui = nil
local safeToggleInstance = nil
local savedOriginalCFrame = nil

local function getSafeGuiParent()
	local parent = nil
	pcall(function()
		if gethui then
			parent = gethui()
		end
	end)
	if not parent then
		pcall(function()
			parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
		end)
	end
	local lp = LocalPlayer or Players.LocalPlayer
	if not parent and lp then
		parent = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
	end
	return parent
end

local function destroySafePlatformUI()
	if safePlatformGui and safePlatformGui.Parent then
		safePlatformGui:Destroy()
	end
	safePlatformGui = nil
end

local function destroySafePlatformPart()
	if platformBedrock and platformBedrock.Parent then
		platformBedrock:Destroy()
	end
	platformBedrock = nil
end

local function ensureSafePlatform()
	if not platformBedrock or not platformBedrock.Parent then
		platformBedrock = Instance.new("Part")
		platformBedrock.Name = "PlatformBedrock"
		platformBedrock.Anchored = true
		platformBedrock.CanCollide = true
		platformBedrock.Size = Vector3.new(8192, PLATFORM_HEIGHT, 8192)
		platformBedrock.Position = Vector3.new(0, PLATFORM_Y, 0)
		platformBedrock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(145, 145, 150)
		platformBedrock.Transparency = 0.55
		platformBedrock.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
		platformBedrock.Parent = workspace
	else
		platformBedrock.Position = Vector3.new(0, PLATFORM_Y, 0)
		platformBedrock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(145, 145, 150)
		platformBedrock.Transparency = 0.55
		platformBedrock.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	end
end

local function teleportToPlatform()
	local lp = LocalPlayer or Players.LocalPlayer
	local char = lp and lp.Character
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hrp then return end

	ensureSafePlatform()

	-- Lưu vị trí ban đầu nếu chưa ở trên nền Y = 4000
	if math.abs(hrp.Position.Y - PLATFORM_SURFACE_Y) > 15 then
		savedOriginalCFrame = hrp.CFrame
	end

	local targetPos = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, PLATFORM_SURFACE_Y, hrp.Position.Z)
	local lookDir = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
	local flatLook = Vector3.new(lookDir.X, 0, lookDir.Z)

	pcall(function()
		hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		if flatLook.Magnitude > 0.001 then
			hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, targetPos + flatLook.Unit)
		else
			hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
		end
	end)

	if type(Message) == "function" then
		pcall(function()
			Message("An toàn", "Đã dịch chuyển lên nền (Y = " .. tostring(PLATFORM_Y) .. ")", 2)
		end)
	end
end

local function teleportBackToOriginal()
	local lp = LocalPlayer or Players.LocalPlayer
	local char = lp and lp.Character
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hrp then return end

	if not savedOriginalCFrame then
		if type(Message) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				Message("An toàn", "Chưa có vị trí ban đầu để quay lại", 2)
			end)
		end
		return
	end

	local targetCFrame = savedOriginalCFrame
	pcall(function()
		hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		hrp.CFrame = targetCFrame
	end)

	if type(Message) == "function" then
		pcall(function()
			Message("An toàn", "Đã quay lại vị trí ban đầu", 2)
		end)
	end
end

local function createSafePlatformUI()
	destroySafePlatformUI()

	local parent = getSafeGuiParent()
	if not parent then return end

	local old = parent:FindFirstChild("SafePlatformYGui")
	if old then old:Destroy() end

	local darkBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(32, 30, 30)
	local borderColor = Color3.fromRGB(95, 90, 90)
	local dividerColor = Color3.fromRGB(115, 110, 110)
	local textLightColor = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)

	local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	screenGui.Name = "SafePlatformYGui"
	screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
	screenGui.DisplayOrder = 955
	screenGui.Parent = parent
	safePlatformGui = screenGui

	local mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	mainFrame.Name = "SafePlatformFrame"
	mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 132, 0, 74)
	mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, 75)
	mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = darkBgColor
	mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
	mainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	mainFrame.Parent = screenGui

	local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
	corner.Parent = mainFrame

	local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	stroke.Color = borderColor
	stroke.Transparency = 0.35
	stroke.Thickness = 1
	stroke.Parent = mainFrame

	local headerBar = Instance.new("Frame")
	headerBar.Name = "HeaderBar"
	headerBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 24)
	headerBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 2)
	headerBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	headerBar.Parent = mainFrame

	local titleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	titleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -1)
	titleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	titleLabel.Text = "An toàn"
	titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	titleLabel.TextSize = 12
	titleLabel.TextColor3 = textLightColor
	titleLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	titleLabel.Parent = headerBar

	local divider = Instance.new("Frame")
	divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
	divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1)
	divider.BackgroundColor3 = dividerColor
	divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
	divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
	divider.Parent = headerBar

	-- Kéo thả di chuyển bảng nhỏ
	do
		local dragging = false
		local dragStart = nil
		local startPos = nil
		local renderConn = nil

		local function stopDrag()
			dragging = false
			dragStart = nil
			startPos = nil
			if renderConn then
				renderConn:Disconnect()
				renderConn = nil
			end
		end

		headerBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				dragging = true
				dragStart = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
				startPos = mainFrame.Position

				if renderConn then
					renderConn:Disconnect()
				end
				renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
					if not dragging or not dragStart or not startPos or not mainFrame.Parent then
						stopDrag()
						return
					end
					local delta = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation() - dragStart
					mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
						startPos.X.Scale,
						startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
						startPos.Y.Scale,
						startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
					)
				end)

				input.Changed:Connect(function()
					if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
						stopDrag()
					end
				end)
			end
		end)

		UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				if dragging then
					stopDrag()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local heartBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	heartBtn.Name = "TeleportHeartBtn"
	heartBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -9, 0, 30)
	heartBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 34)
	heartBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(68, 44, 48)
	heartBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
	heartBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	heartBtn.Text = "❤️"
	heartBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	heartBtn.TextSize = 16
	heartBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	heartBtn.Parent = mainFrame

	local heartCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	heartCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	heartCorner.Parent = heartBtn
