	local heartStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	heartStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(220, 95, 115)
	heartStroke.Transparency = 0.35
	heartStroke.Thickness = 1
	heartStroke.Parent = heartBtn

	local backBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	backBtn.Name = "TeleportBackBtn"
	backBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -9, 0, 30)
	backBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 3, 0, 34)
	backBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 46, 46)
	backBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
	backBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	backBtn.Text = "😜"
	backBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	backBtn.TextSize = 16
	backBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	backBtn.Parent = mainFrame

	local backCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	backCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	backCorner.Parent = backBtn

	local backStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	backStroke.Color = borderColor
	backStroke.Transparency = 0.35
	backStroke.Thickness = 1
	backStroke.Parent = backBtn

	heartBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		teleportToPlatform()
	end)

	backBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		teleportBackToOriginal()
	end)
end

local function setSafePlatformEnabled(enabled)
	local state = (enabled == true)
	platformEnabled = state

	if state then
		destroySafePlatformPart()
		ensureSafePlatform()
		createSafePlatformUI()

		if type(Message) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				Message("An toàn", "Đã bật nền tại Y = " .. tostring(PLATFORM_Y), 2)
			end)
		end
	else
		destroySafePlatformPart()
		destroySafePlatformUI()

		if type(Message) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				Message("An toàn", "Đã tắt nền", 2)
			end)
		end
	end

	if safeToggleInstance and type(safeToggleInstance.Set) == "function" then
		pcall(function()
			safeToggleInstance:Set(state)
		end)
	end

	if type(config) == "table" then
		config.platformEnabled = state
		if type(autoSaveConfig) == "function" then
			pcall(autoSaveConfig)
		end
	end
end

local function togglePlatform()
	setSafePlatformEnabled(not platformEnabled)
end

    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Parent = frame

    label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0)

    label.TextSize = 16
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 180)
    label.Parent = frame

    tip.Parent = playerGui
    task.delay(5, function()
        if tip and tip.Parent then tip:Destroy() end
    end)
end

-- 重新加入当前服务器函数
local function rejoinCurrentServer()
    -- 获取必要的服务
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
    
    -- 验证本地玩家存在
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not localPlayer then
        warn("Không lấy được LocalPlayer")
        return
    end
    
    -- 验证当前在服务器中
    if not game.PlaceId or game.PlaceId <= 0 then
        warn("Không ở trong game hợp lệ")
        return
    end
    
    if not game.JobId or game.JobId == "" then
        warn("Không ở trong máy chủ")
        return
    end
    
    -- 获取当前服务器信息
    local placeId = game.PlaceId
    local jobId = game.JobId
    
    print("=== Thông tin máy chủ ===")
    print("Place ID: ", placeId)
    print("Job ID: ", jobId)
    print("=================")
    
    -- 执行重新加入
    print("Đang gửi yêu cầu vào lại...")
    
    local success, errorMsg = pcall(function()
        -- 核心方法：传送到指定的服务器实例
        TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, jobId, localPlayer)
    end)
    
    if success then
        print("Đã gửi yêu cầu vào lại")
        print("Đang kết nối máy chủ...")
    else
        warn("Vào lại thất bại: ", errorMsg)
        
        -- 失败后尝试备用方法
        print("Đang thử cách khác...")
        
        local backupSuccess, backupError = pcall(function()
            -- 备用方法：普通传送（可能进入新服务器）
            TeleportService:Teleport(placeId, localPlayer)
        end)
        
        if backupSuccess then
            print("Cách khác thành công (có thể vào máy chủ mới)")
        else
            warn("Cách khác cũng thất bại: ", backupError)
        end
    end
end
figureSection:CreateToggle("Emote", function(enabled)
    loadCustomEmotes()
    -- 自动关闭开关
    for _, btn in ipairs(figureSection.Buttons) do
        if btn.Name == "ToggleBtn_Emote" then
            local stateLabel = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
            if stateLabel then
                stateLabel.Text = "Emote"
                stateLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
            end
            break
        end
    end
end)
-- 隐藏和恢复地图功能
local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
local chunks = workspace:FindFirstChild("Chunks")
local bedrock = nil
local mapHidden = false

-- 隐藏地图显示状态
local function toggleMap()
    if mapHidden then
        -- 恢复环境对象
        if map then
            map.Parent = workspace
        end
        if chunks then
            chunks.Parent = workspace
        end
        
        -- 移除基岩
        if bedrock and bedrock.Parent then
            bedrock:Destroy()
            bedrock = nil
        end
        
        Message("Thông báo", "Đã khôi phục bản đồ, xóa nền", 2)
        mapHidden = false
    else
        -- 隐藏环境对象
        if map then
            map.Parent = nil
        end
        if chunks then
            chunks.Parent = nil
        end
        
        -- 生成基岩
        bedrock = Instance.new("Part")
        bedrock.Name = "HiddenEnvironmentBedrock"
        bedrock.Anchored = true
        bedrock.Size = Vector3.new(8192, 2, 8192)
        bedrock.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        bedrock.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("grey")
        bedrock.Parent = workspace
        
        Message("Thông báo", "Đã ẩn bản đồ, tạo nền", 2)
        mapHidden = true
    end
end

-- 平台创建功能
-- 平台创建功能
local platformBedrock = nil
local platformEnabled = (config and config.platformEnabled) or false
local platformY = (config and type(config.platformY) == "number") and config.platformY or 0
local PLATFORM_STEP = 50
local PLATFORM_MAX_Y = 5000
local PLATFORM_MIN_Y = -5000
local PLATFORM_HEIGHT = 2

local platformGui = nil
local updatePlatformYDisplay = nil

local function clampPlatformY(val)
	local num = tonumber(val) or 0
	num = math.clamp(num, PLATFORM_MIN_Y, PLATFORM_MAX_Y)
	-- Làm tròn theo bội số của 50
	return math.floor((num + (num >= 0 and 25 or -25)) / PLATFORM_STEP) * PLATFORM_STEP
end

platformY = clampPlatformY(platformY)

local function syncPlatformPosition()
	if platformBedrock and platformBedrock.Parent then
		platformBedrock.Position = Vector3.new(0, platformY, 0)
		platformBedrock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(145, 145, 150)
		platformBedrock.Transparency = 0.55
		platformBedrock.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	end
	if type(config) == "table" then
		config.platformY = platformY
		if type(autoSaveConfig) == "function" then
			pcall(autoSaveConfig)
		end
	end
	if updatePlatformYDisplay then
		updatePlatformYDisplay()
	end
end

local function teleportToPlatform()
	local lp = LocalPlayer or game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
	local char = lp and lp.Character
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hrp then
		if type(Message) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				Message("Tạo nền", "Không tìm thấy nhân vật để dịch chuyển", 2)
			end)
		end
		return
	end

	local topSurfaceY = platformY + (PLATFORM_HEIGHT * 0.5) + 3.2
	local currentLook = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
	local flatLook = Vector3.new(currentLook.X, 0, currentLook.Z)
	local targetPos = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, topSurfaceY, hrp.Position.Z)

	pcall(function()
		hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		if flatLook.Magnitude > 0.001 then
			hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, targetPos + flatLook.Unit)
		else
			hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
		end
	end)

	if type(Message) == "function" then
		pcall(function()
			Message("Tạo nền", "Đã dịch chuyển lên nền (Y: " .. tostring(platformY) .. ")", 2)
		end)
	end
end

local function destroyPlatformUI()
	if platformGui and platformGui.Parent then
		platformGui:Destroy()
	end
	platformGui = nil
	updatePlatformYDisplay = nil
end

local function getPlatformGuiParent()
	local parent = nil
	pcall(function()
		if gethui then
			parent = gethui()
		end
	end)
	if not parent then
		pcall(function()
			parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
		end)
	end
	if not parent and LocalPlayer then
		parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
	end
	return parent
end

local function createPlatformUI()
	destroyPlatformUI()

	local parent = getPlatformGuiParent()
	if not parent then return end

	local old = parent:FindFirstChild("PlatformYSelectorGui")
	if old then old:Destroy() end

	local darkBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(32, 30, 30)
	local borderColor = Color3.fromRGB(95, 90, 90)
	local dividerColor = Color3.fromRGB(115, 110, 110)
	local btnBgColor = Color3.fromRGB(48, 46, 46)
	local textLightColor = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
	local subTextColor = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 198)

	local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	screenGui.Name = "PlatformYSelectorGui"
	screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
	screenGui.DisplayOrder = 955
	screenGui.Parent = parent
	platformGui = screenGui

	local mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	mainFrame.Name = "PlatformYFrame"
	mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 226, 0, 74)
	mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, 75)
	mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = darkBgColor
	mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
	mainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	mainFrame.Parent = screenGui

	local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
	corner.Parent = mainFrame

	local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	stroke.Color = borderColor
	stroke.Transparency = 0.35
	stroke.Thickness = 1
	stroke.Parent = mainFrame

	local headerBar = Instance.new("Frame")
	headerBar.Name = "HeaderBar"
	headerBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 24)
	headerBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 2)
	headerBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	headerBar.Parent = mainFrame

	local titleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	titleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -1)
	titleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	titleLabel.Text = "Tọa độ Y Nền (±50 | Max 5000)"
	titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	titleLabel.TextSize = 12
	titleLabel.TextColor3 = textLightColor
	titleLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	titleLabel.Parent = headerBar

	local divider = Instance.new("Frame")
	divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
	divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1)
	divider.BackgroundColor3 = dividerColor
	divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
	divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
	divider.Parent = headerBar

	-- Kéo di chuyển bảng nhỏ
	do
		local dragging = false
		local dragStart = nil
		local startPos = nil
		local renderConn = nil

		local function stopDrag()
			dragging = false
			dragStart = nil
			startPos = nil
			if renderConn then
				renderConn:Disconnect()
				renderConn = nil
			end
		end

		headerBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				dragging = true
				dragStart = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
				startPos = mainFrame.Position

				if renderConn then
					renderConn:Disconnect()
				end
				renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
					if not dragging or not dragStart or not startPos or not mainFrame.Parent then
						stopDrag()
						return
					end
					local delta = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation() - dragStart
					mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
						startPos.X.Scale,
						startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
						startPos.Y.Scale,
						startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
					)
				end)

				input.Changed:Connect(function()
					if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
						stopDrag()
					end
				end)
			end
		end)

		UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				if dragging then
					stopDrag()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local minusBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	minusBtn.Name = "MinusYBtn"
	minusBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 30)
	minusBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 34)
	minusBtn.BackgroundColor3 = btnBgColor
	minusBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
	minusBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	minusBtn.Text = "-"
	minusBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	minusBtn.TextSize = 18
	minusBtn.TextColor3 = textLightColor
	minusBtn.Parent = mainFrame

	local minusCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	minusCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	minusCorner.Parent = minusBtn

	local minusStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	minusStroke.Color = borderColor
	minusStroke.Transparency = 0.45
	minusStroke.Thickness = 1
	minusStroke.Parent = minusBtn

	local valueLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	valueLabel.Name = "CurrentYLabel"
	valueLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -122, 0, 30)
	valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 34)
	valueLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 38, 38)
	valueLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
	valueLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
	valueLabel.Text = "Y: " .. tostring(platformY)
	valueLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	valueLabel.TextSize = 14
	valueLabel.TextColor3 = subTextColor
	valueLabel.Parent = mainFrame

	local valCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	valCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	valCorner.Parent = valueLabel

	local plusBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	plusBtn.Name = "PlusYBtn"
	plusBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 30)
	plusBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 34)
	plusBtn.BackgroundColor3 = btnBgColor
	plusBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
	plusBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	plusBtn.Text = "+"
	plusBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	plusBtn.TextSize = 18
	plusBtn.TextColor3 = textLightColor
	plusBtn.Parent = mainFrame

	local plusCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	plusCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	plusCorner.Parent = plusBtn

	local plusStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	plusStroke.Color = borderColor
	plusStroke.Transparency = 0.45
	plusStroke.Thickness = 1
	plusStroke.Parent = plusBtn

	local tpHeartBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	tpHeartBtn.Name = "TeleportPlatformBtn"
	tpHeartBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 30)
	tpHeartBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 34)
	tpHeartBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(68, 44, 48)
	tpHeartBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
	tpHeartBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	tpHeartBtn.Text = "❤️"
	tpHeartBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	tpHeartBtn.TextSize = 15
	tpHeartBtn.TextColor3 = textLightColor
	tpHeartBtn.Parent = mainFrame

	local heartCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	heartCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	heartCorner.Parent = tpHeartBtn

	local heartStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	heartStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(220, 95, 115)
	heartStroke.Transparency = 0.35
	heartStroke.Thickness = 1
	heartStroke.Parent = tpHeartBtn

	updatePlatformYDisplay = function()
		if valueLabel and valueLabel.Parent then
			valueLabel.Text = "Y: " .. tostring(platformY)
			valueLabel.TextColor3 = (platformY == PLATFORM_MAX_Y) and Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 120) or textLightColor
		end
	end

	minusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		platformY = math.clamp(platformY - PLATFORM_STEP, PLATFORM_MIN_Y, PLATFORM_MAX_Y)
		syncPlatformPosition()
	end)

	plusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		platformY = math.clamp(platformY + PLATFORM_STEP, PLATFORM_MIN_Y, PLATFORM_MAX_Y)
		syncPlatformPosition()
	end)

	tpHeartBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		teleportToPlatform()
	end)

	updatePlatformYDisplay()
end

local function togglePlatform()
	if platformEnabled then
		-- Xóa nền & ẩn bảng chọn tọa độ Y
		if platformBedrock and platformBedrock.Parent then
			platformBedrock:Destroy()
			platformBedrock = nil
		end
		destroyPlatformUI()
		platformEnabled = false
		if type(config) == "table" then
			config.platformEnabled = false
			if type(autoSaveConfig) == "function" then
				pcall(autoSaveConfig)
			end
		end
		if type(Message) == "function" then
			Message("Tạo nền", "Đã xóa nền", 2)
		end
	else
		-- Tạo nền màu xám trong suốt & hiện bảng chọn tọa độ Y
		if platformBedrock and platformBedrock.Parent then
			platformBedrock:Destroy()
		end
		platformBedrock = Instance.new("Part")
		platformBedrock.Name = "PlatformBedrock"
		platformBedrock.Anchored = true
		platformBedrock.Size = Vector3.new(8192, PLATFORM_HEIGHT, 8192)
		platformBedrock.Position = Vector3.new(0, platformY, 0)
		platformBedrock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(145, 145, 150)
		platformBedrock.Transparency = 0.55
		platformBedrock.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
		platformBedrock.Parent = workspace

		createPlatformUI()
		syncPlatformPosition()

		platformEnabled = true
		if type(config) == "table" then
			config.platformEnabled = true
			if type(autoSaveConfig) == "function" then
				pcall(autoSaveConfig)
			end
		end
		if type(Message) == "function" then
			Message("Tạo nền", "Đã tạo nền (Y: " .. tostring(platformY) .. ")", 2)
		end
	end
end
-- 其他子菜单
local otherSection = mainUI:NewSection("Khác")
otherSection:CreateToggle("Tạo nền", function(enabled)
    togglePlatform()
end)
otherSection:CreateToggle("Ẩn bản đồ", function(enabled)
    toggleMap()
    -- 自动关闭开关
    for _, btn in ipairs(otherSection.Buttons) do
        if btn.Name == "ToggleBtn_Ẩn bản đồ" then
            local stateLabel = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
            if stateLabel then
                stateLabel.Text = "Ẩn bản đồ"
                stateLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
            end
            break
        end
    end
end)
otherSection:CreateToggle("Vào lại", function(enabled)
    if enabled then
        rejoinCurrentServer()
        -- 自动关闭开关
        for _, btn in ipairs(otherSection.Buttons) do
            if btn.Name == "ToggleBtn_Vào lại máy chủ" then
                local stateLabel = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                if stateLabel then
                    stateLabel.Text = "Vào lại máy chủ"
                    stateLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
                end
                break
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- 持续抓取功能
local function grabPlayerNonStop()
    local args = {
        false,
        false,
        true
    }
    game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Events"):WaitForChild("Grab"):FireServer(unpack(args))

local Players = game:GetService("Players") 
local player = Players.LocalPlayer 
local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait() 
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid") 
local runService = game:GetService("RunService") 
local connection 

connection = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() 
	if character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then 
		local rootPart = character.HumanoidRootPart 
		-- 保持Y轴旋转不变，确保玩家身体直立 
		local currentCFrame = rootPart.CFrame 
		local x, y, z = currentCFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ() 
		local newCFrame = CFrame.new(currentCFrame.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, y, 0) 
		rootPart.CFrame = newCFrame 
	end 
end)
end

local grabUI = nil

local function CreateGrabUI()
    if grabUI then
        grabUI:Destroy()
        grabUI = nil
    end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "GrabUI"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.DisplayOrder = 999
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = "grabButton"
    btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 64)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0.55, 0)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34)
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.Text = "✋"
    btn.TextSize = 32
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.Active = true
    btn.Parent = gui

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    corner.Parent = btn

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 120)
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Parent = btn

    local conns = {}
    local moved = false

    -- 点击执行一次抓取（拖动不算点击）
    table.insert(conns, btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if moved then
            moved = false
            return
        end
        grabPlayerNonStop()
        Message("Tự động bắt", "Đã thực hiện thao tác kéo", 2)
    end))

    -- 拖动逻辑
    local dragging = false
    local dragStart, startPos

    table.insert(conns, btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            moved = false
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = btn.Position
        end
    end))

    table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
            if math.abs(delta.X) > 6 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 6 then
                moved = true
            end
        end
    end))

    table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end))

    gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
            pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
        end
        conns = {}
    end)

    grabUI = gui
    return gui
end

local function ToggleGrabUI()
    if not grabUI then
        CreateGrabUI()
    else
        grabUI:Destroy()
        grabUI = nil
    end
end

otherSection:CreateToggle("Tự động bắt", function(enabled)
    ToggleGrabUI()
    -- 自动关闭开关
    for _, btn in ipairs(otherSection.Buttons) do
        if btn.Name == "ToggleBtn_Tự động bắt" then
            local stateLabel = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
            if stateLabel then
                stateLabel.Text = "Tự động bắt"
                stateLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
            end
            break
        end
    end
end)

-- 绑定F键执行持续抓取
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if not processed and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F then
        grabPlayerNonStop()
        Message("Tự động bắt", "Đã thực hiện thao tác kéo", 2)
    end
end)

-- ===================== 事件绑定 =====================
-- 角色重生处理
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
    task.wait(0.5)
    

    
    -- 掉落拉回状态恢复
    if antiFallEnabled then
        startAntiFall(newCharacter)
    end
end)



-- 初始化
getgenv().FPDH = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
Message("Đã tải giao diện", "Đã tải xong trợ giúp Eat the World", 3)
